    }

    if (latestAssistantId !== p.baselineAssistantId) {
      const beforeReanchor = { ...p, items: clonePendingItems(p.items) };
      try {
        await reanchorAfterResponse(room, latestAssistant, retryingSameAssistant ? { countTurn:false, reason:'recovery' } : {});
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[RP매니저] reanchor failed:', room.chatId, e);
        // 실패 시 이전/새 carrier 양쪽이 깨끗한지 확인합니다. 네트워크 오류로 확인하지 못했다면
        // pending과 이중 백업을 남겨 다음 recoveryTick 또는 수동 해제로 재시도할 수 있게 합니다.
        let cleanupConfirmed = true;
        for (const messageId of [...new Set([beforeReanchor.messageId, latestAssistantId].filter(Boolean))]) {
          try { await restoreCarrierOnly(room, { ...beforeReanchor, messageId }); }
          catch (cleanupError) {
            cleanupConfirmed = false;
            console.warn('[RP매니저] reanchor cleanup not confirmed:', messageId, cleanupError);
          }
        }
        const failedCurrent = room.pending;
        const failedItems = clonePendingItems(failedCurrent?.items || p.items || beforeReanchor.items);
        const activeAfterFailure = activePendingItems({ items:failedItems });
        if (activeAfterFailure.length) {
          const attempts = retryingSameAssistant ? Number(recovery?.attempts || 0) + 1 : 1;
          const holdUntilNextAssistant = /안전선을 넘어/.test(String(e.message || '')) || attempts >= 3;
          const preserved = cleanupConfirmed
            ? { ...beforeReanchor, items:failedItems }
            : { ...(failedCurrent || beforeReanchor), items:failedItems };
          preserved.verified = false;
          preserved.verifiedAt = null;
          preserved.reanchorRetry = {
            failedMessageId:String(latestAssistantId),
            failedAt:Date.now(),
            attempts,
            nextRetryAt:Date.now() + 15000,
            holdUntilNextAssistant,
            reason:String(e.message || 'carrier 자동 이동 실패'),
          };
          room.pending = preserved;
          savePendingBackup(room.chatId, preserved);
        } else if (cleanupConfirmed) {
          room.pending = null;
          clearPendingBackup(room.chatId);
        } else {
          room.pending = failedCurrent || beforeReanchor;
          savePendingBackup(room.chatId, room.pending);
        }
        await saveRoom(room);
        if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
          state.currentRoom = room;
          if (room.pending) notify(`자동 이동 보류 · 계속 유지 설정과 주입 항목을 보존했습니다. ${room.pending.reanchorRetry?.holdUntilNextAssistant ? '다음 AI 답변에서 다시 시도합니다.' : '잠시 후 자동으로 다시 시도합니다.'} ${e.message}`, 'warn', 8500);
          else if (cleanupConfirmed) notifyInjectionEnded(room, 'error', e.message);
          else notify(`자동 이동 오류 · 복구 확인 전이라 주입 백업을 유지했습니다: ${e.message}`, 'error', 7500);
          renderModalIfOpen();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async function recoveryTick() {
    if (state.recovering || state.rerollPreparing || !state.db) return;
    state.recovering = true;
    try {
      // 화면에 없는 모든 방을 getAll()로 복제하지 않습니다. 현재 보고 있는 방만
      // 검사하고, 다른 방의 pending은 그 방에 다시 들어갔을 때 이어서 복구합니다.
      const room = state.currentRoom;
      if (!room || room.chatId !== state.currentChatId) return;
      try {
        normalizeRoomSlots(room);
        // 자동 캐릭터·중요 물품 감지는 주입 전에도 현재 방에서 동작해 다음 주입 준비를 해둡니다.
        const hasAutoItems = room.autoItemDetection && (room.slots || []).some(slot => slot.group === 'item' && !slot.autoExcluded && String(slot.content || '').trim());
        if (!room.pending && (room.autoCharacterDetection || room.autoLogRecallEnabled || hasAutoItems)) {
          const lastScanAt = Number(state.idleAutoScanAt.get(room.chatId) || 0);
          if (Date.now() - lastScanAt < APP.idleAutoScanMs) return;
          state.idleAutoScanAt.set(room.chatId, Date.now());
          const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), APP.autoScanMessageLimit);
          const auto = await refreshAutomaticMemories(room, recent);
          if (auto.detected?.length && room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
            notify(`RP 등장 캐릭터 자동 선택 · ${auto.detected.map(x => x.slot.title).join(', ')} 현재 주입 활성화`, 'success', 3800);
            renderModalIfOpen();
          }
          if (auto.itemDetected?.length && room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
            notify(`중요 물품 언급 감지 · ${auto.itemDetected.map(x => x.slot.title).join(', ')} 현재 주입 활성화`, 'success', 4200);
            renderModalIfOpen();
          }
          return;
        }
        if (!room.pending) return;
        if (!Array.isArray(room.pending.items) || !room.pending.items.length) {
          const legacyTotal = room.pending.totalTurns == null ? APP.defaultRetentionTurns : normalizeRetentionTurns(room.pending.totalTurns);
          const legacyUsed = Number(room.pending.usedTurns || 0);
          room.pending.items = selectedSlots(room).map(slot => ({ slotId: slot.id, title: slot.title, group: slot.group, content: String(slot.content || ''), totalTurns: normalizeRetentionTurns(slot.retentionTurns ?? legacyTotal), usedTurns: legacyUsed }));
        }
        await checkPendingRoom(room);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[RP매니저] pending check failed:', room.chatId, e);
      }
    } finally {
      state.recovering = false;
    }
  }

  function nextRecoveryDelay() {
    if (document.hidden) return APP.backgroundPollMs;
    if (state.currentRoom?.pending) return APP.activePollMs;
    if (state.currentRoom?.autoCharacterDetection || state.currentRoom?.autoLogRecallEnabled) return APP.idleAutoScanMs;
    return APP.idlePollMs;
  }

  function scheduleRecovery(delay = nextRecoveryDelay()) {
    clearTimeout(state.recoveryTimer);
    state.recoveryTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      state.recoveryTimer = null;
      try { await recoveryTick(); }
      catch (e) { console.warn('[RP매니저] recovery loop failed', e); }
      scheduleRecovery();
    }, Math.max(0, Number(delay) || 0));
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Render-only sanitizer: 서버 raw는 유지하고 일반 채팅 화면에서만 RP 블록 숨김
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  function cleanupRenderedMarkerArtifacts(root) {
    if (!root) return;
    const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT | NodeFilter.SHOW_COMMENT);
    const removeNodes = []; let n;
    while ((n = walker.nextNode())) {
      if (n.nodeType === Node.COMMENT_NODE) {
        if (String(n.nodeValue || '').includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER')) removeNodes.push(n);
        continue;
      }
      if (n.parentElement?.closest('#rpcm-overlay,#rpcm-toast-wrap,#rpcm-notice-popups,#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')) continue;
      const before = n.nodeValue || '';
      // 🪽위시 RP Manager는 자기 마커가 포함된 흔적만 정리합니다.
      // 빈 <!----> 자체는 Crack/다른 확장이 사용할 수 있으므로 전역 삭제하지 않습니다.
      const beforeNorm = String(before || '');
      const after = /RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER(?:_START|_END)?/i.test(beforeNorm)
        ? beforeNorm.replace(/\?<!--?[^\n]*RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER[^\n]*>?/gi, '').replace(/^[\\\s]+$/g, m => m.includes('\\') ? m.replace(/\\/g, '') : m)
        : beforeNorm;
      if (after !== before) n.nodeValue = after;
    }
    removeNodes.forEach(x => x.remove());
  }

  function sanitizeOneRenderedBlock(startNode) {
    if (!startNode || startNode.nodeType !== Node.TEXT_NODE) return false;
    if (startNode.parentElement?.closest('#rpcm-overlay,#rpcm-toast-wrap,#rpcm-notice-popups,#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')) return false;
    let root = startNode.parentElement;
    for (let i = 0; root && i < 12; i++, root = root.parentElement) {
      if (root.id === 'rpcm-overlay' || root.id === 'rpcm-toast-wrap' || root.id === 'rpcm-notice-popups' || root.id === 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop') return false;
      const text = root.textContent || '';
      if (!text.includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_END')) continue;

      const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
      const nodes = []; let joined = ''; let n;
      while ((n = walker.nextNode())) {
        if (n.parentElement?.closest('#rpcm-overlay,#rpcm-toast-wrap,#rpcm-notice-popups,#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')) continue;
        nodes.push({ node: n, start: joined.length, end: joined.length + n.nodeValue.length });
        joined += n.nodeValue;
      }
      let start = joined.indexOf('<!--RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_START');
      if (start < 0) start = joined.indexOf('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_START');
      let end = joined.indexOf('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_END-->', start >= 0 ? start : 0);
      let endLen = 'RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_END-->'.length;
      if (end < 0) { end = joined.indexOf('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_END', start >= 0 ? start : 0); endLen = 'RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_END'.length; }
      if (start < 0 || end < 0) continue;
      // Markdown renderer가 HTML 주석 앞의 escape 백슬래시를 따로 남기는 경우까지 같이 제거합니다.
      if (start > 0 && joined[start - 1] === '\\') start--;
      const cutEnd = end + endLen;

      for (const part of nodes) {
        if (part.end <= start || part.start >= cutEnd) continue;
        const localStart = Math.max(0, start - part.start);
        const localEnd = Math.min(part.node.nodeValue.length, cutEnd - part.start);
        part.node.nodeValue = part.node.nodeValue.slice(0, localStart) + part.node.nodeValue.slice(localEnd);
      }
      cleanupRenderedMarkerArtifacts(root);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function isManagerUiNode(node) {
    const element=node?.nodeType===Node.ELEMENT_NODE?node:node?.parentElement;
    return !!element?.closest?.('[id^="rpcm-"],[id^="wish-rpcore-"],script,style,textarea');
  }
  function renderedContextWalker(root) {
    return document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_ELEMENT|NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT|NodeFilter.SHOW_COMMENT,{
      acceptNode:node=>node.nodeType===Node.ELEMENT_NODE?(isManagerUiNode(node)?NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT:NodeFilter.FILTER_SKIP):NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT
    });
  }
  function sanitizeRenderedContextBlocks(root = document.body) {
    if (!root || isManagerUiNode(root)) return;
    const walker = renderedContextWalker(root);
    const starts = [];
    const comments = [];
    let node;
    while ((node = walker.nextNode())) {
      const v = node.nodeValue || '';
      if (!v.includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER')) continue;
      if (node.nodeType === Node.COMMENT_NODE) {
        comments.push(node);
        continue;
      }
      if (node.parentElement?.closest('#rpcm-overlay,#rpcm-toast-wrap,#rpcm-notice-popups,#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')) continue;
      if (v.includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER_START')) starts.push(node);
    }
    // Markdown이 실제 HTML 주석으로 만든 경우에는 4만 자짜리 Comment 노드 자체를 제거합니다.
    comments.forEach(comment => comment.remove());
    for (const start of starts) sanitizeOneRenderedBlock(start);
  }

  function sanitizeRenderedContextSoon() {
    clearTimeout(state.domSanitizeTimer);
    state.domSanitizeTimer = setTimeout(() => sanitizeRenderedContextBlocks(document.body || document.documentElement || document), 0);
  }

  function nodeContainsRenderedContextMarker(node) {
    if (!node || isManagerUiNode(node)) return false;
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE || node.nodeType === Node.COMMENT_NODE) {
      return String(node.nodeValue || '').includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER');
    }
    if (!node.ownerDocument && node !== document) return false;
    const walker = renderedContextWalker(node);
    let child;
    while ((child = walker.nextNode())) {
      if (String(child.nodeValue || '').includes('RP_CONTEXT_MANAGER')) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function startRenderedContextObserver() {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    const target = document.documentElement || document;
    if (!state.domObserver) {
      state.domObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
        if (state.domSanitizing || document.hidden) return;
        const roots = new Set();
        for (const m of mutations) {
          if (isManagerUiNode(m.target)) continue;
          invalidateAiMemoryHistory();
          if (m.type === 'characterData') {
            if (nodeContainsRenderedContextMarker(m.target)) roots.add(m.target.parentElement || m.target);
            continue;
          }
          // mutation.target의 textContent는 채팅 전체 문자열을 새로 만들 수 있으므로 읽지 않습니다.
          // 실제로 추가된 노드만 검사해 스트리밍 중 반복되는 전체 DOM 직렬화를 막습니다.
          for (const added of (m.addedNodes || [])) {
            if (!nodeContainsRenderedContextMarker(added)) continue;
            if (added.nodeType === Node.COMMENT_NODE) {
              added.remove();
            } else {
              roots.add(added.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE ? added : (added.parentElement || m.target));
            }
          }
        }
        if (!roots.size) return;
        state.domSanitizing = true;
        try {
          for (const root of roots) sanitizeRenderedContextBlocks(root);
        } finally {
          state.domSanitizing = false;
        }
      });
    }
    if (!state.domObserverActive) {
      state.domObserver.observe(target, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
      state.domObserverActive = true;
    }
    sanitizeRenderedContextBlocks(document.body || document.documentElement || document);
  }

  function pauseRenderedContextObserver() {
    if (!state.domObserver || !state.domObserverActive) return;
    state.domObserver.disconnect();
    state.domObserverActive = false;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Mobile viewport / layout helpers
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  function isMobileManagerLayout() {
    try {
      const narrow = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 680px)').matches;
      const touchTablet = window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse) and (max-width: 1024px)').matches;
      return narrow || touchTablet;
    } catch (_) { return window.innerWidth <= 680; }
  }

  function updateViewportMetrics() {
    const vv = window.visualViewport;
    const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(vv?.height || window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 1));
    const top = Math.max(0, Math.round(vv?.offsetTop || 0));
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (!root) return;
    const mobile = isMobileManagerLayout();
    if (root.classList.contains('rpcm-mobile-layout') !== mobile) root.classList.toggle('rpcm-mobile-layout', mobile);
    if (!mobile) state.mobileViewportMaxHeight = 0;
    else if (height > state.mobileViewportMaxHeight) state.mobileViewportMaxHeight = height;
    if (root.style.getPropertyValue('--rpcm-vvh') !== `${height}px`) root.style.setProperty('--rpcm-vvh', `${height}px`);
    if (root.style.getPropertyValue('--rpcm-vv-top') !== `${top}px`) root.style.setProperty('--rpcm-vv-top', `${top}px`);
    const activeInput = document.activeElement;
    const typingFocus = !!activeInput?.matches?.('textarea,input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="file"]):not([type="hidden"])') && !!activeInput.closest?.('#rpcm-overlay,#rpcm-detached-backdrop,#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop,#rpcm-library-manager-backdrop,#rpcm-log-dialog-backdrop,#rpcm-story-dialog-backdrop,#rpcm-story-view-backdrop,#rpcm-dup-dialog-backdrop,#rpcm-import-backdrop,#rpcm-ai-backdrop');
    const keyboardOpen = mobile && typingFocus && state.mobileViewportMaxHeight > 0 && height < state.mobileViewportMaxHeight * .82;
    if (root.classList.contains('rpcm-mobile-keyboard-open') !== keyboardOpen) root.classList.toggle('rpcm-mobile-keyboard-open', keyboardOpen);
  }

  function bindViewportMetrics() {
    updateViewportMetrics();
    if (state.viewportMetricsBound) return;
    state.viewportMetricsBound = true;
    const refresh = () => {
      updateViewportMetrics();
      // 세로/가로 전환으로 680px 경계를 넘나들 때 진입 버튼도 즉시 재배치합니다.
      ensureManagerButton();
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', refresh, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => {
      state.mobileViewportMaxHeight = 0;
      setTimeout(refresh, 80);
      setTimeout(refresh, 320);
    }, { passive: true });
    if (window.visualViewport) {
      window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', updateViewportMetrics, { passive: true });
      window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll', updateViewportMetrics, { passive: true });
    }
  }

  function responseRefinerConnectionInfo(){
    const shared=aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),'utility');
    let stored={};try{stored=JSON.parse(GM_getValue('wish-rp-core-response-refiner-settings-v3','')||'{}');}catch(_){}
    const live=window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_REFINER__?.connectionInfo?.();
    return {...shared,provider:shared.provider,model:String(live?.model||stored.responseRefinerModels?.[shared.provider]||shared.models?.[shared.provider]||''),thinkingLevel:live?.thinkingLevel||stored.responseRefinerThinkingLevel||'medium',enabled:live?.enabled??stored.enabled??true};
  }

  function openUnifiedApiSettingsDialog(room, mount = null) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      if (!mount) document.querySelector('#rpcm-api-settings-backdrop')?.remove();
      let settings = loadAiSummarySettings();
      let provider = settings.provider;
      let semanticKeyDraft=readSemanticApiKey(),semanticResult=null,semanticBusy=false;
      const rememberSemantic=()=>{const input=backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-semantic-key');if(input)semanticKeyDraft=input.value.trim();};
      const semanticFeedback=(kind,message)=>{
        const node=backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-semantic-status');if(!node)return;
        node.dataset.state=kind;node.textContent=message;
      };
      const draftSecrets = new Map(AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDERS.map(id => [id, readAiSecret(id)]));
      const backdrop = mount || document.createElement('div');
      if (!mount) { backdrop.id = 'rpcm-api-settings-backdrop'; backdrop.className = 'rpcm-unified-api-backdrop'; document.body.appendChild(backdrop); }
      else mount.hidden=false;
      const statusText = status => {
        if (!status) return '<span class="rpcm-api-state idle">● 확인 전</span>';
        const label = status.state === 'ok' ? '● 정상' : status.state === 'off' ? '● OFF' : '● 오류';
        const detail = String(status.detail || '');
        return `<span class="rpcm-api-state ${esc(status.state || 'idle')}">${label}</span>${status.message ? `<small>${esc(status.message)}</small>` : ''}${status.state === 'error' && detail ? `<small class="rpcm-api-error-detail" title="${esc(detail)}">${esc(detail.slice(0, 600))}</small>` : ''}`;
      };
      const rememberForm = () => {
        rememberSemantic();
        const providerEl = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-provider');
        const formProvider = provider;
        const secretEl = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-secret');
        if (secretEl) draftSecrets.set(formProvider, secretEl.value);
        const featureModels = { ...(settings.featureModels || {}) };
        for (const feature of ['summary','timeline','context','itemFolder','utility']) {
          const control=backdrop.querySelector(`[data-api-feature-model="${feature}"]`);if(!control)continue;
          const model = String(control.value || settings.models?.[formProvider] || AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[formProvider]?.model || '');
          featureModels[feature] = { provider:formProvider, model };
        }
        settings.featureModels = featureModels;
        settings.models = { ...settings.models, [formProvider]:featureModels.summary?.model || settings.models?.[formProvider] || '' };
        const url = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-openai-url');
        const location = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-vertex-location');
        const project = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-vertex-project');
        if (url) settings.openaiBaseUrl = String(url.value || '').trim();
        if (location) settings.vertexLocation = String(location.value || 'global').trim() || 'global';
        if (project) settings.vertexProjectId = String(project.value || '').trim();
        const contextToggle = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-context-enabled');
        if (contextToggle) room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled = contextToggle.checked;
        provider = AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDERS.includes(providerEl?.value) ? providerEl.value : formProvider;
        settings.provider = provider;
      };
      const persist = async () => {
        rememberForm();
        settings = saveAiSummarySettings(settings);
        // 빈 draft 값 때문에 이미 저장된 인증정보가 삭제되지 않도록 보호합니다.
        // 인증정보 삭제는 별도 UI가 생기기 전까지 자동으로 수행하지 않습니다.
        for (const [id, value] of draftSecrets) {
          if (String(value || '').trim()) writeAiSecret(id, value);
        }
        await saveRoom(room);
      };
      const render = () => {
        const models = AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider] || [];
        const modelOptions = selected => {
          const fallback = AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider]?.model || models[0]?.value || '';
          const safeSelected = models.some(item => item.value === selected) ? selected : fallback;
          return models.map(item => `<option value="${esc(item.value)}" ${item.value === safeSelected ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('');
        };
        const featureRow = (feature, description) => {
          const featureSetting = settings.featureModels?.[feature];
          const selected = featureSetting?.provider === provider
            ? String(featureSetting.model || '')
            : String(settings.models?.[provider] || AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider]?.model || models[0]?.value || '');
          const status = settings.featureStatus?.[feature];
          const modelControl = provider === 'openai'
            ? `<input data-api-feature-model="${feature}" list="rpcm-openai-model-list" value="${esc(selected)}" placeholder="모델 ID 직접 입력">`
            : `<select data-api-feature-model="${feature}">${modelOptions(selected)}</select>`;
          return `<div class="rpcm-api-feature-row"><div><strong>${esc(aiFeatureStatusLabel(feature))}</strong><small>${esc(description)}</small></div>${modelControl}<div class="rpcm-api-feature-status">${statusText(feature === 'context' && !room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled ? { state:'off' } : status)}</div></div>`;
        };
        const refiner=responseRefinerConnectionInfo();
        const refinerStatus=settings.featureStatus?.responseRefiner;
        const refinerStatusCurrent=refinerStatus?.provider===refiner.provider&&refinerStatus?.model===refiner.model?refinerStatus:null;
        const refinerRow=`<div class="rpcm-api-feature-row"><div><strong>AI 응답교정</strong><small>실제 교정 연결 · ${refiner.enabled?'자동 검토 ON':'자동 검토 OFF · 수동 검토 가능'}</small></div><div><span>${esc(refiner.provider)} · ${esc(refiner.model)}</span><small>추론 ${esc(refiner.thinkingLevel)} · 짧은 연결 테스트는 교정 품질 검사가 아닙니다.</small><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="refiner-settings">교정 설정</button></div><div class="rpcm-api-feature-status">${statusText(refinerStatusCurrent)}</div></div>`;
        const semanticCard=`<section class="rpcm-ai-card" data-semantic-connection><h3>의미검색 전용 연결</h3><p class="rpcm-ai-help">의미 검색용 Gemini API Key. Firebase 방식은 검색 준비용 별도 키가 필요합니다. <a href="https://aistudio.google.com/apikey" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="rpcm-ai-studio-link" title="Google AI Studio에서 API 키 발급" aria-label="Google AI Studio에서 API 키 발급"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M14 3h7v7M21 3l-11 11M11 5H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v12a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6"/></svg></a>.</p><label class="rpcm-ai-field"><span>검색용 Gemini API 키</span><input id="rpcm-api-semantic-key" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" spellcheck="false" value="${esc(semanticKeyDraft)}" placeholder="의미검색에만 사용할 키" aria-label="의미검색용 Gemini API 키"></label><p class="rpcm-ai-help">검색용 키는 공용 연결과 따로 저장됩니다. AI 요약·응답교정 등은 위의 공용 연결을 사용합니다. 키 발급은 무료이며 API 이용 한도·요금은 프로젝트 설정에 따라 달라집니다.</p><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="semantic-save" ${semanticBusy?'disabled':''}>검색용 키 저장</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="semantic-test" ${semanticBusy?'disabled':''}>의미검색 연결 테스트</button></div><div id="rpcm-api-semantic-status" class="rpcm-ai-status" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></section>`;
        const openAiModelList = provider === 'openai'
          ? `<datalist id="rpcm-openai-model-list">${models.map(item => `<option value="${esc(item.value)}">${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('')}</datalist>`
          : '';
        const openAiModelHelp = provider === 'openai'
          ? `<p class="rpcm-ai-help">목록에 없는 OpenAI 호환 모델 ID도 직접 입력할 수 있습니다. 주소는 /v1 또는 /chat/completions까지 입력해도 자동 처리합니다.</p>`
          : '';
        const providerOptions = AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDERS.map(id => `<option value="${id}" ${id === provider ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[id].label)}</option>`).join('');
        const secretLabel = provider === 'firebase' ? 'Firebase 설정 코드' : provider === 'vertex' ? 'Vertex 서비스 계정 JSON' : 'API 키';
        const secretInput = provider === 'firebase' || provider === 'vertex'
          ? `<textarea id="rpcm-api-secret" spellcheck="false" placeholder="${esc(secretLabel)}">${esc(draftSecrets.get(provider) || '')}</textarea>`
          : `<input id="rpcm-api-secret" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" value="${esc(draftSecrets.get(provider) || '')}" placeholder="${esc(secretLabel)}">`;
        backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-ai-dialog rpcm-unified-api-dialog"><div class="rpcm-ai-head"><div><h2>⚙ API 설정</h2><p>기능별 AI 연결과 응답교정의 실제 모델·상태를 확인합니다.</p></div><div class="rpcm-ai-spacer"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-close" data-api-act="close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div><div class="rpcm-ai-body"><section class="rpcm-ai-card"><h3>공통 연결</h3><div class="rpcm-ai-grid"><label class="rpcm-ai-field"><span>연결 방식</span><select id="rpcm-api-provider">${providerOptions}</select></label>${provider === 'openai' ? `<label class="rpcm-ai-field"><span>OpenAI / 호환 API 주소</span><input id="rpcm-api-openai-url" value="${esc(settings.openaiBaseUrl || '')}" placeholder="https://api.openai.com/v1"></label>` : ''}${provider === 'vertex' ? `<label class="rpcm-ai-field"><span>Vertex 위치</span><input id="rpcm-api-vertex-location" value="${esc(settings.vertexLocation || 'global')}"></label><label class="rpcm-ai-field"><span>프로젝트 ID</span><input id="rpcm-api-vertex-project" value="${esc(settings.vertexProjectId || '')}"></label>` : ''}<label class="rpcm-ai-field rpcm-ai-secret" style="grid-column:1/-1"><span>${secretLabel}</span>${secretInput}</label></div>${provider==='firebase'?wishAppCheckControlsHtml():''}<p class="rpcm-ai-help">인증 정보는 이 브라우저에만 저장되며 전체 백업에는 포함되지 않습니다.</p><div class="rpcm-api-common-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="test">공용 AI 연결 테스트</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn primary" data-api-act="save">공용 설정 저장</button></div><p class="rpcm-ai-help">요약·장기기억·응답교정은 공용 연결을 사용합니다. 아래 의미검색 연결은 검색만 별도로 설정합니다.</p><div class="rpcm-ai-status" data-api-common-status aria-live="polite"></div></section>${semanticCard}<section class="rpcm-ai-card"><h3>기능별 모델과 상태</h3><div class="rpcm-api-feature-list">${featureRow('summary','날짜요약·현재상태 생성')}${featureRow('timeline','전체 타임라인 초안 생성')}${featureRow('context','관련 날짜로그 후보 검토')}${featureRow('itemFolder','중요 물품 폴더 제안')}${featureRow('utility','검색·검수·제안 기능 공통')}${refinerRow}</div>${openAiModelList}${openAiModelHelp}<label class="rpcm-api-context-toggle"><span><strong>AI 맥락 검토 사용</strong><small>실패하면 기본 키워드 방식으로 자동 대체합니다.</small></span><input id="rpcm-api-context-enabled" type="checkbox" ${room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled ? 'checked' : ''}></label></section>${renderRoomAiUsageHtml(room)}<div class="rpcm-ai-status" id="rpcm-api-settings-status"></div></div><div class="rpcm-ai-foot"><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="test">공용 AI 연결 테스트</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" data-api-act="close">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn primary" data-api-act="save">공용 설정 저장</button></div></div>`;
        backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-semantic-key').oninput=()=>{rememberSemantic();semanticResult=null;semanticFeedback('idle','키를 변경했습니다. 저장하거나 연결을 확인해 주세요.');};
        if(semanticBusy)semanticFeedback('idle','의미검색 연결을 확인하는 중…');
        else if(semanticResult&&semanticResult.key===semanticKeyDraft)semanticFeedback(semanticResult.kind,semanticResult.message);
        else semanticFeedback('idle',semanticKeyDraft?'Gemini 임베딩 · gemini-embedding-001 · 연결 확인 전':'검색용 키를 입력해 주세요. 키가 없으면 로컬 검색을 사용합니다.');
        backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-provider').onchange = event => { rememberForm(); provider = event.target.value; settings.provider = provider; render(); };
      };
      const finish = value => { if(mount){mount.replaceChildren();mount.hidden=true;}else backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.addEventListener('click', async event => {
        if (event.target === backdrop) { finish(false); return; }
        const action = event.target.closest('[data-api-act]')?.dataset.apiAct;
        if (!action) return;
        if (action === 'close') { finish(false); return; }
        if(action==='appcheck-token'){await wishCopyAppCheckFromUi(backdrop,backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-secret')?.value||'');return;}
        if (action === 'refiner-settings') {
          finish(false);
          const refiner=window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_REFINER__;
          if(typeof refiner?.openSettings==='function')refiner.openSettings();
          else notify('응답교정 모듈이 초기화되지 않았습니다. 페이지를 새로고침한 뒤 다시 시도해 주세요.','error',5500);
          return;
        }
        if(action==='semantic-save'||action==='semantic-test'){
          if(semanticBusy)return;rememberSemantic();const key=semanticKeyDraft;
          try{
            if(!key)throw new Error('의미검색용 Gemini API 키를 입력해 주세요.');
            if(action==='semantic-save'){
              saveSemanticApiKey(key);semanticResult={key,kind:'ok',message:'검색용 키 저장됨 · 공용 연결 설정은 그대로 유지됩니다.'};
            }else{
              semanticBusy=true;backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-api-act="semantic-save"],[data-api-act="semantic-test"]').forEach(button=>button.disabled=true);
              semanticFeedback('idle','의미검색 연결을 확인하는 중…');const started=Date.now();
              const prompt='의미검색 연결 확인',result=await semanticApiRequest(key,[prompt],'RETRIEVAL_QUERY',SEMANTIC_SEARCH.indexTimeoutMs);
              recordRoomAiUsage(room,'semanticQuery','gemini',SEMANTIC_SEARCH.model,{inputTokens:result.tokens,outputTokens:0},{},{prompt});refreshAiRoomUsageUi(room);queueRoomAutoSave(room,800);
              semanticResult={key,kind:'ok',message:`연결 정상 · 단일 요청 성공 (실제 검색 준비의 한도는 별도) · 256차원 · ${((Date.now()-started)/1000).toFixed(1)}초 · ${readAiSecret('semanticGemini').trim()===key?'검색용 키 저장됨':'입력한 키로 테스트했습니다. 사용하려면 검색용 키를 저장해 주세요.'}`};
            }
          }catch(error){semanticResult={key,kind:'error',message:`${action==='semantic-save'?'저장':'연결 테스트'} 실패 · ${String(error.message||'다시 시도해 주세요.').replaceAll(key||'__empty_key__','[키 숨김]')}`};}
          finally{semanticBusy=false;if(backdrop.isConnected){backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-api-act="semantic-save"],[data-api-act="semantic-test"]').forEach(button=>button.disabled=false);if(semanticKeyDraft===key)semanticFeedback(semanticResult.kind,semanticResult.message);}}
          return;
        }
        if (action === 'save') { try{await persist();notify('API 설정을 저장했습니다.', 'success');finish(true);}catch(error){backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-settings-status').textContent=String(error.message||'설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');}return; }
        if (action === 'test') {
          const button = event.target.closest('button');
          const status = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-settings-status');
          try {
            await persist(); backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-api-act="test"],[data-api-act="save"]').forEach(el=>el.disabled=true); status.textContent = '기능별 연결을 확인하는 중…';const commonStatus=backdrop.querySelector('[data-api-common-status]');if(commonStatus)commonStatus.textContent=status.textContent;
            for (const feature of ['summary','timeline','context','itemFolder','utility','responseRefiner']) {
              if (feature === 'context' && !room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled) { setAiFeatureStatus(feature, 'off', provider, settings.featureModels?.[feature]?.model, '사용 안 함'); continue; }
              const next = feature==='responseRefiner'?responseRefinerConnectionInfo():aiFeatureSettings(settings, feature);
              const model = feature==='responseRefiner'?next.model:next.models?.[next.provider] || '';
              const started=Date.now();
              try {
                const result = feature==='responseRefiner'
                  ? await window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_AI_BRIDGE__.call('연결 테스트입니다. 정확히 OK만 출력하세요.',{test:true,responseRefiner:true,model,thinkingLevel:next.thinkingLevel})
                  : await callAiSummaryProvider(next.provider, next, draftSecrets.get(next.provider) || '', '짧게 응답하세요.', '연결 테스트입니다. 정확히 OK만 출력하세요.', 64, () => {}, { feature, room });
                setAiFeatureStatus(feature, 'ok', next.provider, result.model || model, `연결 및 모델 호출 정상 · ${((Date.now()-started)/1000).toFixed(1)}초`);
              } catch (error) {
                const failure = classifyAiFailure(error);
                setAiFeatureStatus(feature, 'error', next.provider, model, failure.category, failure.detail);
              }
            }
            settings = loadAiSummarySettings(); render(); backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-settings-status').textContent = '기능별 연결 테스트를 마쳤습니다. 각 항목의 정상·오류 상태를 확인해 주세요.';backdrop.querySelector('[data-api-common-status]').textContent=backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-api-settings-status').textContent;
          } catch (error) { status.textContent = `테스트 실패 · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`;const commonStatus=backdrop.querySelector('[data-api-common-status]');if(commonStatus)commonStatus.textContent=status.textContent; }
          finally { backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-api-act="test"],[data-api-act="save"]').forEach(el=>el.disabled=false); }
        }
      });
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') finish(false); };
      render();
    });
  }

  function openAiSummaryDialog(room) {
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-backdrop')?.remove();
    const settings = loadAiSummarySettings();
    const draftSecrets = new Map(AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDERS.map(id => [id, readAiSecret(id)]));
    let currentProvider = settings.provider;
    const savedDraft = room.aiSummaryDraft && typeof room.aiSummaryDraft === 'object' ? room.aiSummaryDraft : null;
    let generatedDraft = savedDraft && (String(savedDraft.logSummary || '') || String(savedDraft.currentState || '')) ? {
      logSummary:String(savedDraft.logSummary || ''),
      currentState:String(savedDraft.currentState || ''),
      baseCurrentState:String(savedDraft.baseCurrentState ?? aiFixedSlot(room, 'currentState')?.content ?? ''),
      lastTurnKeys:{ logSummary:String(savedDraft.lastTurnKeys?.logSummary || ''), currentState:String(savedDraft.lastTurnKeys?.currentState || '') },
      createdAt:String(savedDraft.createdAt || savedDraft.savedAt || nowIso()),
      savedAt:String(savedDraft.savedAt || ''),
      provider:String(savedDraft.provider || ''),
      model:String(savedDraft.model || ''),
      usage:savedDraft.usage && typeof savedDraft.usage === 'object' ? { ...savedDraft.usage } : {},
      warnings:Array.isArray(savedDraft.warnings) ? [...savedDraft.warnings] : [],
      turns:[],
      historyId:String(savedDraft.historyId || ''),
      savedTurnCount:Math.max(0, Number(savedDraft.turnCount) || 0),
      savedTargets:savedDraft.targets && typeof savedDraft.targets === 'object' ? { ...savedDraft.targets } : null,
      savedSelections:savedDraft.selections && typeof savedDraft.selections === 'object' ? { ...savedDraft.selections } : null,
      resumeBefore:savedDraft.resumeBefore && typeof savedDraft.resumeBefore === 'object' ? structuredClone(savedDraft.resumeBefore) : null,
      freshLogHeadings:Array.isArray(savedDraft.freshLogHeadings) ? savedDraft.freshLogHeadings.map(String) : [],

    } : null;

            <div class="rpcm-ai-generate-row"><div class="rpcm-ai-status" id="rpcm-ai-status"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn primary" id="rpcm-ai-generate">지금 요약 생성</button></div>
            </div>
          </section>
          <div class="rpcm-ai-results" id="rpcm-ai-results" hidden>
            <div class="rpcm-ai-run-usage" id="rpcm-ai-run-usage"></div>
            <div class="rpcm-ai-tabs"><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-tab active" id="rpcm-ai-tab-log">날짜요약</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-tab" id="rpcm-ai-tab-state">현재상태</button></div>
            <section class="rpcm-ai-card rpcm-ai-result is-active" id="rpcm-ai-log-result-card"><div class="rpcm-ai-result-head"><label><input id="rpcm-ai-apply-log" type="checkbox" checked> <strong>날짜요약 결과를 적용에 포함</strong></label><div class="rpcm-ai-spacer"></div><button class="rpcm-ai-btn" id="rpcm-ai-copy-log" type="button">복사</button></div><div class="rpcm-ai-new-blocks" id="rpcm-ai-new-blocks" hidden></div><textarea id="rpcm-ai-log-result" spellcheck="false"></textarea><div class="rpcm-ai-validation" id="rpcm-ai-log-validation"></div></section>
            <section class="rpcm-ai-card rpcm-ai-result" id="rpcm-ai-state-result-card"><div class="rpcm-ai-result-head"><label><input id="rpcm-ai-apply-state" type="checkbox" checked> <strong>현재상태 결과를 적용에 포함</strong></label><div class="rpcm-ai-spacer"></div><button class="rpcm-ai-btn" id="rpcm-ai-copy-state" type="button">복사</button></div><div class="rpcm-ai-view-tabs"><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-view-tab active" id="rpcm-ai-state-edit-tab">결과 편집</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-view-tab" id="rpcm-ai-state-diff-tab">기존 상태와 변경 비교</button></div><textarea id="rpcm-ai-state-result" spellcheck="false"></textarea><div class="rpcm-ai-diff" id="rpcm-ai-state-diff" hidden></div><div class="rpcm-ai-validation" id="rpcm-ai-state-validation"></div></section>
          </div>
        </main>
        <footer class="rpcm-ai-foot"><span class="rpcm-ai-usage" id="rpcm-ai-usage"></span><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" id="rpcm-ai-undo" ${room.aiSummaryUndo ? '' : 'disabled'}>마지막 적용 되돌리기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn" id="rpcm-ai-cancel" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="text">닫기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-btn primary" id="rpcm-ai-apply" disabled>선택 결과 적용</button></footer>
      </section>`;
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    state.aiSummaryDialog = backdrop;

    // 호스트 페이지가 details/summary뿐 아니라 section의 auto 높이까지 덮어쓰는 경우가 있어
    // 일반 section+button을 쓰고 닫혔을 때만 제목 높이를 고정합니다. 열린 본문은 auto로 두어
    // 안쪽 설정을 다시 펼쳐도 부모와 스크롤 영역이 곧바로 실제 높이를 반영하게 합니다.
    let aiDisclosureFrame = 0;
    const aiDisclosureContainers = () => [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-collapsible]')];
    const measureAiDisclosure = container => {
      if (!container?.querySelector) return;
      const trigger = container.querySelector(':scope > .rpcm-ai-disclosure-trigger');
      if (!trigger?.getBoundingClientRect) return;
      const triggerHeight = Math.max(38, Math.ceil(trigger.getBoundingClientRect().height || trigger.offsetHeight || 0));
      if (container.dataset.aiOpen !== 'true') {
        container.style.setProperty('height', `${triggerHeight}px`, 'important');
        return;
      }
      // 호스트 페이지가 section의 auto 높이를 제목 높이로 다시 덮어쓰는 경우가 있어
      // 열린 직계 본문의 실제 높이를 더한 픽셀값을 부모에 강제로 적용합니다.
      // ResizeObserver와 두 번의 RAF 측정으로 안쪽 접기 영역이 바뀔 때도 다시 계산합니다.
      const bodies = [...container.children].filter(child => child !== trigger && !child.hidden);
      const bodyHeight = bodies.reduce((sum, body) => {
        const rectHeight = Math.ceil(body.getBoundingClientRect?.().height || 0);
        const scrollHeight = Math.ceil(Number(body.scrollHeight) || 0);
        return sum + Math.max(rectHeight, scrollHeight);
      }, 0);
      container.style.setProperty('height', `${Math.max(triggerHeight, triggerHeight + bodyHeight)}px`, 'important');
    };
    const refreshAiDisclosureHeights = () => {
      aiDisclosureFrame = 0;
      // 안쪽 비용/사용량 블록부터 계산해야 바깥 API 설정 블록 높이에 정확히 포함됩니다.
      aiDisclosureContainers().reverse().forEach(measureAiDisclosure);
    };
    const scheduleAiDisclosureHeights = () => {
      if (aiDisclosureFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(aiDisclosureFrame);
      aiDisclosureFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        refreshAiDisclosureHeights();
        aiDisclosureFrame = requestAnimationFrame(refreshAiDisclosureHeights);
      });
    };
    const setAiDisclosureOpen = (container, open) => {
      if (!container?.matches?.('[data-ai-collapsible]')) return;
      const trigger = container.querySelector(':scope > .rpcm-ai-disclosure-trigger');
      if (!trigger) return;
      const isOpen = !!open;
      container.classList.toggle('is-open', isOpen);
      container.dataset.aiOpen = isOpen ? 'true' : 'false';
      [...container.children].filter(child => child !== trigger).forEach(body => {
        body.hidden = !isOpen;
        if (isOpen) {
          body.style.setProperty('position', 'static', 'important');
          body.style.setProperty('inset', 'auto', 'important');
          body.style.setProperty('float', 'none', 'important');
          body.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
          body.style.setProperty('visibility', 'visible', 'important');
          body.style.setProperty('opacity', '1', 'important');
        }
      });
      trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
      const disclosure = trigger.querySelector('[data-ai-disclosure]');
      if (disclosure) disclosure.textContent = isOpen
        ? String(disclosure.dataset.openLabel || '접기 ▴')
        : String(disclosure.dataset.closedLabel || '열기 ▾');
      scheduleAiDisclosureHeights();
    };
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-collapsible]').forEach(container => {
      setAiDisclosureOpen(container, container.dataset.aiOpen === 'true');
    });
    backdrop.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const trigger = event.target?.closest?.('.rpcm-ai-disclosure-trigger') || null;
      if (!trigger || !backdrop.contains(trigger)) return;
      const container = trigger.parentElement;
      if (!container?.matches?.('[data-ai-collapsible]')) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      setAiDisclosureOpen(container, container.dataset.aiOpen !== 'true');
    }, true);
    const aiDisclosureResizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'
      ? new ResizeObserver(scheduleAiDisclosureHeights)
      : null;
    aiDisclosureContainers().forEach(container => {
      const trigger = container.querySelector(':scope > .rpcm-ai-disclosure-trigger');
      if (trigger) aiDisclosureResizeObserver?.observe(trigger);
      [...container.children].filter(child => child !== trigger).forEach(body => aiDisclosureResizeObserver?.observe(body));
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', scheduleAiDisclosureHeights, { passive:true });
    scheduleAiDisclosureHeights();

    const $ = selector => backdrop.querySelector(selector);
    const providerEl = $('#rpcm-ai-provider');
    const modelEl = $('#rpcm-ai-model');
    const secretHost = $('#rpcm-ai-secret-host');
    const statusEl = $('#rpcm-ai-status');
    const resultsEl = $('#rpcm-ai-results');
    const applyBtn = $('#rpcm-ai-apply');
    const generateBtn = $('#rpcm-ai-generate');
    const testBtn = $('#rpcm-ai-test');
    const usageEl = $('#rpcm-ai-usage');
    const totalUsageEl = $('#rpcm-ai-total-usage');
    const turnCountEl = $('#rpcm-ai-turns');
    const inputPreviewEl = $('#rpcm-ai-input-preview');
    let currentInputPlan = null;
    let inputPreviewTurns = null;
    let inputPreviewTimer = 0;
    let inputPreviewSequence = 0;
    const historyDetailsEl = $('#rpcm-ai-history');
    const historySelectEl = $('#rpcm-ai-history-select');
    const historyTextEl = $('#rpcm-ai-history-text');
    let activeHistoryTarget = 'logSummary';
    const historyDateLabel = value => {
      const date = new Date(value);
      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? '저장 시각 미상' : date.toLocaleString('ko-KR', { year:'numeric', month:'numeric', day:'numeric', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' });
    };
    const historyCoverageLabel = entry => {
      const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(String(entry?.logSummary || ''));
      const last = [...blocks].reverse().find(block => !block.isUnknown) || blocks[blocks.length - 1];
      return last?.heading || '';
    };
    const renderAiSummaryHistory = preferredId => {
      const history = normalizeAiSummaryHistory(room);
      historyDetailsEl.hidden = !history.length;
      $('#rpcm-ai-history-count').textContent = `${formatCount(history.length)}개 저장됨`;
      if (!history.length) {
        historySelectEl.innerHTML = '';
        historyTextEl.value = '';
        $('#rpcm-ai-history-meta').textContent = '';
        return;
      }
      const previousId = String(preferredId || historySelectEl.value || history[0].id);
      historySelectEl.innerHTML = history.map((entry, index) => {
        const coverage = historyCoverageLabel(entry);
        const label = `${index + 1}. ${coverage ? `${coverage}까지` : historyDateLabel(entry.updatedAt || entry.createdAt)} · ${entry.turnCount ? `${formatCount(entry.turnCount)}턴` : '턴 수 미상'} · ${entry.model || entry.provider || '모델 미상'}`;
        return `<option value="${esc(entry.id)}">${esc(label)}</option>`;
      }).join('');
      historySelectEl.value = history.some(entry => entry.id === previousId) ? previousId : history[0].id;
      const selected = history.find(entry => entry.id === historySelectEl.value) || history[0];
      const value = String(selected?.[activeHistoryTarget] || '');
      historyTextEl.value = value || `이 기록에는 ${activeHistoryTarget === 'logSummary' ? '날짜요약' : '현재상태'} 결과가 없습니다.`;
      const usage = selected.usage || {};
      const applied = selected.appliedAt ? ` · 적용 ${historyDateLabel(selected.appliedAt)}` : ' · 미적용/적용 여부 미기록';
      const cost = Number.isFinite(Number(usage.estimatedCostUsd)) ? ` · 예상 ${formatAiCostUsd(usage.estimatedCostUsd)}` : '';
      const coverage = historyCoverageLabel(selected);
      $('#rpcm-ai-history-meta').textContent = `${coverage ? `${coverage}까지 요약 · ` : ''}${selected.provider || '제공자 미상'} · ${selected.model || '모델 미상'} · ${formatCount(selected.turnCount)}턴${cost}${applied}`;
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-history-target]').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('active', button.dataset.aiHistoryTarget === activeHistoryTarget));
    };
    historySelectEl.onchange = () => renderAiSummaryHistory(historySelectEl.value);
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-history-target]').forEach(button => {

    });
    $('#rpcm-ai-history-copy').onclick = async () => {

      notify(value && await copyPlainText(value) ? '이전 생성 결과를 복사했습니다.' : '복사할 결과가 없습니다.', value ? 'success' : 'warn', 2600);
    };
    renderAiSummaryHistory(generatedDraft?.historyId || '');
    const providerHelp = {
      gemini:'Google AI Studio에서 발급한 Gemini API 키를 사용합니다.',
      firebase:'',
      vertex:'고급 방식입니다. Google Cloud 서비스 계정 JSON 전체를 붙여넣으세요. Vertex AI 권한과 결제가 필요할 수 있습니다.',
      deepseek:'DeepSeek API 키를 사용합니다.',
      openai:'OpenAI 또는 /chat/completions 형식의 호환 서버 주소·키·모델을 사용합니다.',
    };
    const updateUsage = () => {
      const usage = loadAiSummaryUsage();
      const coverage = usage.calls > usage.pricedCalls ? ` · 비용 기록 ${formatCount(usage.pricedCalls)}/${formatCount(usage.calls)}회` : '';
      const text = `누적 API 호출 ${formatCount(usage.calls)}회${usage.inputTokens || usage.outputTokens ? ` · 입력 ${formatCount(usage.inputTokens)} / 출력 ${formatCount(usage.outputTokens)} 토큰` : ''} · 누적 예상 비용 ${formatAiCostUsd(usage.estimatedCostUsd)}${coverage}`;
      usageEl.textContent = `누적 API ${formatCount(usage.calls)}회 · 예상 ${formatAiCostUsd(usage.estimatedCostUsd)}`;
      usageEl.title = text;
      totalUsageEl.textContent = `${text}\n예상액은 선택한 모델의 안내 단가 기준이며 무료 할당량·크레딧·환율·세금은 반영하지 않습니다.`;
    };
    const updateConnectionSummary = () => {
      const provider = String(providerEl.value || settings.provider || '');
      const model = String(modelEl.value || settings.models?.[provider] || '');
      const secret = String(draftSecrets.get(provider) || '').trim();
      const dot = $('#rpcm-ai-connection-state');
      dot?.classList.toggle('ready', !!secret);
      $('#rpcm-ai-connection-summary').textContent = secret
        ? `${AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider]?.label || provider} · ${model || '모델 미선택'} · 눌러서 변경`
        : `${AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider]?.label || provider} · 연결 정보 필요`;
    };
    const refreshResumeInfo = () => {
      const info = aiSummaryResumeInfo(room);
      const label = $('#rpcm-ai-resume-label');
      const help = $('#rpcm-ai-resume-help');
      if (label) label.textContent = info.label;
      if (help) help.textContent = info.help;
    };
    let resumeRefreshSequence = 0;
    const refreshResumeProgress = async () => {
      const progress = $('#rpcm-ai-resume-progress');
      if (!progress) return;
      const keys = {
        ...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(generatedDraft?.lastTurnKeys || {}).filter(([, key]) => String(key || ''))),
        ...(room.aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKeys || {}),
      };
      if (!keys.logSummary && room.aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKey) keys.logSummary = room.aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKey;
      if (!keys.currentState && room.aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKey) keys.currentState = room.aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKey;
      const selectedTargets = [
        ['logSummary','날짜요약', !!$('#rpcm-ai-target-log')?.checked],
        ['currentState','현재상태', !!$('#rpcm-ai-target-state')?.checked],
      ].filter(([, , selected]) => selected);
      if (!selectedTargets.length) {
        progress.textContent = '생성할 항목을 선택하면 저장된 기준 이후 턴 수를 확인합니다.';
        return;
      }
      if (!selectedTargets.some(([id]) => keys[id])) {
        progress.textContent = '첫 적용 또는 리셋 뒤부터 여기에서 진행 턴 수를 표시합니다.';
        return;
      }
      const sequence = ++resumeRefreshSequence;
      progress.textContent = '저장된 기준 이후 완료 대화 턴 수를 확인하는 중…';
      try {
        const chatId = apiChatIdOf(room);
        if (!chatId) throw new Error('현재 채팅방 ID를 찾지 못했습니다.');
        const history = await fetchAiMessageHistory(chatId, '', 500);
        const turns = buildCompletedAiDialogueTurns(history);
        if (sequence !== resumeRefreshSequence || !document.contains(backdrop)) return;
        inputPreviewTurns = turns;
        const counts = new Map();
        const labels = selectedTargets.map(([id, label]) => {
          if (!keys[id]) return `${label} 첫 적용 전`;
          const count = aiTurnsAfterSavedKey(turns, keys[id]);
          counts.set(id, count);
          return count < 0 ? `${label} 기준점이 최근 범위에 없음` : `${label} ${formatCount(count)}턴`;
        });
        let suffix = '';
        const canAutoFit = $('#rpcm-ai-new-only')?.checked
          && selectedTargets.every(([id]) => keys[id] && Number(counts.get(id)) >= 0);
        if (canAutoFit) {
          const pendingTurns = Math.max(...selectedTargets.map(([id]) => Number(counts.get(id)) || 0));
          const nextValue = Math.min(100, pendingTurns);
          setTurnCount(nextValue);
          suffix = pendingTurns > 100
            ? ` · 입력값 100턴으로 자동 설정 · 미처리 ${formatCount(pendingTurns)}턴 중 오래된 100턴부터 처리`
            : ` · 입력값을 ${formatCount(nextValue)}턴으로 자동 설정`;
        }
        progress.textContent = `저장된 이어 요약 기준 이후 완료 대화 · ${labels.join(' · ')}${suffix}`;
      } catch (error) {
        if (sequence !== resumeRefreshSequence || !document.contains(backdrop)) return;
        progress.textContent = `진행 턴 수 확인 실패 · ${error.message}`;
      }
    };
    const renderPricing = (provider, model) => {
      const pricing = getAiPricing(settings, provider, model);
      $('#rpcm-ai-price-help').innerHTML = `<strong>선택 모델 단가 안내 · 읽기 전용</strong>\n입력 ${pricing.inputPerMillion == null ? '정보 없음' : '$'+pricing.inputPerMillion+' / 100만 토큰'} · 출력 ${pricing.outputPerMillion == null ? '정보 없음' : '$'+pricing.outputPerMillion+' / 100만 토큰'} · ${esc(pricing.source)}`;
    };
    const rememberVisibleSecret = () => {
      const input = $('#rpcm-ai-secret');
      if (input) draftSecrets.set(currentProvider, input.value);
      settings.models[currentProvider] = String(modelEl.value || '').trim();
    };
    const renderProvider = provider => {
      currentProvider = provider;
      const presets = AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider] || [];
      const requested = settings.models[provider] || AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider].model || presets[0]?.value || '';
      const allowCustomModel = provider === 'openai' && requested && !presets.some(item => item.value === requested);
      const selected = allowCustomModel || presets.some(item => item.value === requested)
        ? requested
        : (AI_SUMMARY_PROVIDER_DEFAULTS[provider].model || presets[0]?.value || '');
      modelEl.innerHTML = `${allowCustomModel ? `<option value="${esc(requested)}">${esc(requested)} · 사용자 지정</option>` : ''}${presets.map(item => `<option value="${esc(item.value)}">${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('')}`;
      modelEl.value = selected;
      settings.models[provider] = selected;
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-ai-openai').forEach(el => { el.hidden = provider !== 'openai'; });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-ai-vertex').forEach(el => { el.hidden = provider !== 'vertex'; });
      const large = provider === 'firebase' || provider === 'vertex';
      $('#rpcm-ai-secret-label').textContent = provider === 'firebase' ? 'Firebase 설정 코드' : provider === 'vertex' ? 'Vertex 서비스 계정 JSON' : 'API 키';
      secretHost.innerHTML = large
        ? `<textarea id="rpcm-ai-secret" spellcheck="false" placeholder="${provider === 'firebase' ? 'firebaseConfig 또는 전체 <script> 코드' : '서비스 계정 JSON'}"></textarea>`
        : '<input id="rpcm-ai-secret" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="이 브라우저에만 별도 저장">';
      $('#rpcm-ai-secret').value = draftSecrets.get(provider) || '';
      if(provider==='firebase'){secretHost.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',wishAppCheckControlsHtml());secretHost.querySelector('[data-api-act=appcheck-token]').onclick=()=>wishCopyAppCheckFromUi(secretHost,$('#rpcm-ai-secret')?.value||'');}
      $('#rpcm-ai-provider-help').textContent = providerHelp[provider] || '';
