      updateConnectionSummary();
      scheduleInputPreview();
    };
    providerEl.onchange = () => {
      rememberVisibleSecret();
      settings.provider = providerEl.value;
      renderProvider(settings.provider);
    };
    modelEl.onchange = () => {
      settings.models[currentProvider] = String(modelEl.value || '').trim();
      renderPricing(currentProvider, settings.models[currentProvider]);
      updateConnectionSummary();
      scheduleInputPreview();
    };

    const guideTextEl = $('#rpcm-ai-guide-text');
    const guideStatusEl = $('#rpcm-ai-guide-status');
    const guideSummaryStatusEl = $('#rpcm-ai-guide-summary-status');
    const guideVariantEl = $('#rpcm-ai-log-guide-variant');
    let activeGuideId = 'logSummary';
    let activeLogGuideVariant = getLogSummaryGuideVariant(true);
    let guideLoaded = false;
    let guideSaveTimer = 0;
    if (guideVariantEl) guideVariantEl.value = activeLogGuideVariant;
    const refreshAiGuideModifiedState = (statusMessage = '') => {
      const activeVariant = activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '';
      const activeModified = isAiSystemInstructionModified(activeGuideId, activeVariant, guideTextEl?.value);
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-guide]').forEach(button => {
        const slotId = button.dataset.aiGuide;
        const variant = slotId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '';
        const modified = slotId === activeGuideId
          ? activeModified
          : isAiSystemInstructionModified(slotId, variant);
        button.classList.toggle('is-modified', modified);
      });
      const anyModified = isAiSystemInstructionModified('currentState')
        || isAiSystemInstructionModified('logSummary', 'general')
        || isAiSystemInstructionModified('logSummary', 'adult')
        || activeModified;
      if (guideSummaryStatusEl) guideSummaryStatusEl.textContent = anyModified
        ? '사용자가 수정한 API 지침이 있습니다.'
        : '기본 지침과 API 보강 지침을 한 번에 확인·수정';
      if (guideSummaryStatusEl) guideSummaryStatusEl.classList.toggle('is-modified', anyModified);
      const label = activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? `날짜요약 ${logSummaryGuideVariantLabel(activeLogGuideVariant)}` : '현재상태';
      if (guideStatusEl) guideStatusEl.textContent = statusMessage || `${label} · ${activeModified ? '수정됨' : '기본값'} · API 호출에 사용되는 전체본`;
      if (guideStatusEl) guideStatusEl.classList.toggle('is-modified', activeModified);
      return activeModified;
    };
    const saveVisibleGuide = () => {
      if (!guideTextEl) return;
      saveAiSystemInstruction(activeGuideId, guideTextEl.value, activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '');
      refreshAiGuideModifiedState(`자동 저장됨 · ${isAiSystemInstructionModified(activeGuideId, activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '', guideTextEl.value) ? '수정됨' : '기본값'} · 다음 API 요약부터 사용됩니다.`);
    };
    const showGuide = slotId => {
      if (!BASE_GUIDES[slotId]) return;
      if (guideSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer); guideSaveTimer = 0; }
      if (guideLoaded && activeGuideId !== slotId) saveVisibleGuide();
      activeGuideId = slotId;
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-guide]').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('active', button.dataset.aiGuide === slotId));
      if (guideVariantEl) {
        guideVariantEl.hidden = slotId !== 'logSummary';
        guideVariantEl.value = activeLogGuideVariant;
      }
      guideTextEl.value = getAiSystemInstruction(slotId, slotId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '');
      guideLoaded = true;
      refreshAiGuideModifiedState();
    };
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-guide]').forEach(button => { button.onclick = () => showGuide(button.dataset.aiGuide); });
    if (guideVariantEl) guideVariantEl.onchange = () => {
      if (guideSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer); guideSaveTimer = 0; }
      if (activeGuideId === 'logSummary' && guideLoaded) saveVisibleGuide();
      activeLogGuideVariant = saveLogSummaryGuideVariant(guideVariantEl.value, true);
      guideVariantEl.value = activeLogGuideVariant;
      guideTextEl.value = getAiSystemInstruction('logSummary', activeLogGuideVariant);
      refreshAiGuideModifiedState();
    };
    guideTextEl.oninput = () => {
      refreshAiGuideModifiedState('저장 중…');
      if (guideSaveTimer) clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer);
      guideSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => { guideSaveTimer = 0; saveVisibleGuide(); }, 300);
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-guide-copy').onclick = async () => notify(await copyPlainText(guideTextEl.value) ? 'API 요약 지침 전체를 복사했습니다.' : '복사에 실패했습니다.', 'success', 2600);
    $('#rpcm-ai-guide-reset').onclick = () => {
      const label = activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? `날짜요약 ${logSummaryGuideVariantLabel(activeLogGuideVariant)}` : '현재상태';
      if (!confirm(`${label} API 요약 지침 전체를 기본값으로 복원할까요? API 화면에서 직접 수정한 내용은 사라집니다.`)) return;
      guideTextEl.value = resetAiSystemInstruction(activeGuideId, activeGuideId === 'logSummary' ? activeLogGuideVariant : '');
      refreshAiGuideModifiedState(`${label} 기본값으로 복원했습니다.`);
    };
    showGuide('logSummary');

    const normalizeTurnCount = (value, fallback = 30) => {
      const digits = String(value ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '');
      const parsed = Number.parseInt(digits, 10);
      const fallbackNumber = Number(fallback);
      const safe = Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : Number.isFinite(fallbackNumber) ? fallbackNumber : 30;
      return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(safe)));
    };
    const updateTurnPresetState = () => {
      const value = normalizeTurnCount(turnCountEl.value, settings.turnCount);
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-turn-preset]').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('active', Number(button.dataset.turnPreset) === value));
    };
    const setTurnCount = value => {
      turnCountEl.value = String(normalizeTurnCount(value, settings.turnCount));
      updateTurnPresetState();
      scheduleInputPreview();
    };
    turnCountEl.oninput = () => {
      turnCountEl.value = String(turnCountEl.value || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 3);
      updateTurnPresetState();
      scheduleInputPreview();
    };
    turnCountEl.onblur = () => setTurnCount(turnCountEl.value);
    turnCountEl.onkeydown = event => {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowUp' && event.key !== 'ArrowDown') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      setTurnCount(normalizeTurnCount(turnCountEl.value, settings.turnCount) + (event.key === 'ArrowUp' ? 1 : -1));
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-turn-minus').onclick = () => setTurnCount(normalizeTurnCount(turnCountEl.value, settings.turnCount) - 1);
    $('#rpcm-ai-turn-plus').onclick = () => setTurnCount(normalizeTurnCount(turnCountEl.value, settings.turnCount) + 1);
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-turn-preset]').forEach(button => { button.onclick = () => setTurnCount(Number(button.dataset.turnPreset)); });

    const collectSettings = persist => {
      rememberVisibleSecret();
      settings.provider = providerEl.value;
      settings.models[settings.provider] = String(modelEl.value || '').trim();
      settings.openaiBaseUrl = String($('#rpcm-ai-openai-url').value || '').trim();
      settings.vertexLocation = String($('#rpcm-ai-vertex-location').value || 'global').trim() || 'global';
      settings.vertexProjectId = String($('#rpcm-ai-vertex-project').value || '').trim();
      settings.turnCount = normalizeTurnCount(turnCountEl.value, 30);
      turnCountEl.value = String(settings.turnCount);
      settings.preferUnprocessed = $('#rpcm-ai-new-only').checked;
      settings.targets = { logSummary:$('#rpcm-ai-target-log').checked, currentState:$('#rpcm-ai-target-state').checked };
      if (persist) {
        settings.featureModels = { ...(loadAiSummarySettings().featureModels || {}), summary:{ provider:settings.provider, model:String(settings.models?.[settings.provider] || '') } };
        saveAiSummarySettings(settings);
        // AI 요약 창도 빈 캐시값으로 기존 인증정보를 지우지 않습니다.
        for (const [id, value] of draftSecrets) {
          if (String(value || '').trim()) writeAiSecret(id, value);
        }
      }
      return settings;
    };
    const renderCallNote = next => {
      const calls = Number(!!next.targets?.logSummary) + Number(!!next.targets?.currentState);
      $('#rpcm-ai-call-note').innerHTML = calls
        ? `<strong>${calls}회 호출</strong> · ${next.targets.logSummary ? '날짜요약' : ''}${next.targets.logSummary && next.targets.currentState ? ' + ' : ''}${next.targets.currentState ? '현재상태' : ''}`
        : '<strong>생성 항목을 선택해 주세요.</strong>';
    };
    const renderInputPlan = plan => {
      currentInputPlan = plan;
      const risk = plan.risk;
      const noTurns = plan.selectedTurns < 1;
      inputPreviewEl.className = `rpcm-ai-progress rpcm-ai-input-preview ${noTurns ? 'empty' : risk.level}`;
      const percent = noTurns ? 0 : Math.min(100, Math.round(plan.maxChars / AI_SUMMARY_MAX_INPUT_CHARS * 100));
      const targetName = targetId => targetId === 'logSummary' ? '날짜요약' : '현재상태';
      const targetLabels = plan.details.map(detail => `${targetName(detail.targetId)} ${formatCount(detail.chars)}자`).join(' · ');
      const dialogueCounts = [...new Set(plan.details.map(detail => Number(detail.breakdown?.dialogueChars || 0)))];
      const dialogueLabel = dialogueCounts.length === 1
        ? `선택 RP 로그 ${formatCount(dialogueCounts[0])}자`
        : `선택 RP 로그 · ${plan.details.map(detail => `${targetName(detail.targetId)} ${formatCount(detail.breakdown?.dialogueChars || 0)}자`).join(' · ')}`;
      const breakdownLabels = plan.details.map(detail => `${targetName(detail.targetId)} 구성 · 지침 ${formatCount(detail.breakdown?.guideChars || 0)}자 · 기존 기억 ${formatCount(detail.breakdown?.memoryChars || 0)}자 · 요청 안내 ${formatCount(detail.breakdown?.wrapperChars || 0)}자`).join('<br>');
      const safeTurns = plan.recommendedMaxTurns > 0 ? `${formatCount(plan.recommendedMaxTurns)}턴` : '현재 저장 내용만으로도 권장량 초과';
      const cost = plan.estimatedCostUsd == null ? '단가 정보 없음' : `약 ${formatAiCostUsd(plan.estimatedCostUsd)}`;
      const rangeMessage = noTurns ? '새로 요약할 완료 대화가 없습니다.' : risk.message;
      const headline = noTurns ? '새 로그 없음' : risk.label;
      const meterHelp = noTurns
        ? '새 RP 로그가 0자라 막대를 비웠습니다. API 총입력 숫자는 지침·기존 기억·요청 안내의 합계입니다.'
        : `위험도 막대는 두 호출 중 총입력이 큰 쪽을 기준으로 합니다. 권장 ${formatCount(AI_SUMMARY_RECOMMENDED_INPUT_CHARS)}자 · 주의 ${formatCount(AI_SUMMARY_CAUTION_INPUT_CHARS)}자 · 차단 ${formatCount(AI_SUMMARY_MAX_INPUT_CHARS)}자.`;
      inputPreviewEl.innerHTML = `<strong>${headline} · 실제 선택 ${formatCount(plan.selectedTurns)}턴 · ${dialogueLabel}</strong><br>API 총입력 · ${targetLabels}<div class="rpcm-ai-meter"><span style="width:${percent}%"></span></div>${rangeMessage}<br>현재 RP 로그 길이 기준 권장 최대 ${safeTurns} · 예상 ${plan.calls}회 호출 · 비용 ${cost}<br><span class="rpcm-ai-help">${breakdownLabels}<br>${meterHelp}</span>`;
      generateBtn.dataset.inputBlocked = risk.level === 'blocked' || noTurns ? 'true' : 'false';
      if (!state.aiSummaryBusy) generateBtn.disabled = risk.level === 'blocked' || noTurns;
    };
    const refreshInputPreview = async () => {
      const sequence = ++inputPreviewSequence;
      try {
        const next = collectSettings(false);
        renderCallNote(next);
        inputPreviewEl.className = 'rpcm-ai-progress rpcm-ai-input-preview';
        inputPreviewEl.textContent = '선택 범위의 실제 입력량을 계산하는 중…';
        if (!next.targets.logSummary && !next.targets.currentState) {
          currentInputPlan = null;
          generateBtn.dataset.inputBlocked = 'true';
          if (!state.aiSummaryBusy) generateBtn.disabled = true;
          inputPreviewEl.textContent = '날짜요약 또는 현재상태를 하나 이상 선택해 주세요.';
          return;
        }
        if (!inputPreviewTurns) {
          const chatId = apiChatIdOf(room);
          if (!chatId) throw new Error('현재 채팅방 ID를 찾지 못했습니다.');
          const history = await fetchAiMessageHistory(chatId, '', 500);
          inputPreviewTurns = buildCompletedAiDialogueTurns(history);
        }
        if (sequence !== inputPreviewSequence || !document.contains(backdrop)) return;
        renderInputPlan(calculateAiSummaryInputPlan(aiRoomWithPendingDraft(room, generatedDraft), next, inputPreviewTurns));
      } catch (error) {
        if (sequence !== inputPreviewSequence || !document.contains(backdrop)) return;
        currentInputPlan = null;
        inputPreviewEl.className = 'rpcm-ai-progress rpcm-ai-input-preview caution';
        inputPreviewEl.textContent = `입력량을 미리 계산하지 못했습니다. 생성 전 범위를 확인해 주세요. · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`;
        generateBtn.dataset.inputBlocked = 'false';
        if (!state.aiSummaryBusy) generateBtn.disabled = false;
      }
    };
    const scheduleInputPreview = () => {
      if (inputPreviewTimer) clearTimeout(inputPreviewTimer);
      inputPreviewTimer = setTimeout(() => { inputPreviewTimer = 0; refreshInputPreview(); }, 420);
    };
    const setBusy = (busy, message = '', tone = '') => {
      state.aiSummaryBusy = busy;
      generateBtn.disabled = busy || generateBtn.dataset.inputBlocked === 'true';
      testBtn.disabled = busy;
      $('#rpcm-ai-save-settings').disabled = busy;
      if (busy) applyBtn.disabled = true;
      statusEl.textContent = message;
      statusEl.className = `rpcm-ai-status${tone ? ` ${tone}` : ''}`;
    };
    let draftSaveTimer = 0;
    let draftClearedAfterApply = false;
    let closing = false;
    const persistGeneratedDraft = async () => {
      if (!generatedDraft || draftClearedAfterApply) return;
      generatedDraft.logSummary = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value;
      generatedDraft.currentState = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value;
      upsertAiSummaryHistory(room, generatedDraft);
      const targets = {
        logSummary:!$('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden,
        currentState:!$('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden,
      };
      room.aiSummaryDraft = {
        version:2,
        logSummary:generatedDraft.logSummary,
        currentState:generatedDraft.currentState,
        baseCurrentState:String(generatedDraft.baseCurrentState ?? aiFixedSlot(room, 'currentState')?.content ?? ''),
        lastTurnKeys:{
          logSummary:String(generatedDraft.lastTurnKeys?.logSummary || ''),
          currentState:String(generatedDraft.lastTurnKeys?.currentState || ''),
        },
        createdAt:String(generatedDraft.createdAt || nowIso()),
        savedAt:nowIso(),
        provider:String(generatedDraft.provider || settings.provider || ''),
        model:String(generatedDraft.model || settings.models?.[settings.provider] || ''),
        usage:generatedDraft.usage && typeof generatedDraft.usage === 'object' ? { ...generatedDraft.usage } : {},
        warnings:Array.isArray(generatedDraft.warnings) ? [...generatedDraft.warnings] : [],
        historyId:String(generatedDraft.historyId || ''),
        turnCount:Math.max(0, Number(generatedDraft.savedTurnCount) || Number(generatedDraft.turns?.length) || 0),
        resumeBefore:generatedDraft.resumeBefore && typeof generatedDraft.resumeBefore === 'object' ? structuredClone(generatedDraft.resumeBefore) : null,
        freshLogHeadings:Array.isArray(generatedDraft.freshLogHeadings) ? generatedDraft.freshLogHeadings.map(String) : [],
        continuedFromDraft:!!generatedDraft.continuedFromDraft,
        targets,
        selections:{
          logSummary:targets.logSummary && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').checked,
          currentState:targets.currentState && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').checked,
        },
      };
      await saveRoom(room);
    };
    const queueDraftSave = () => {
      if (!generatedDraft) return;
      draftClearedAfterApply = false;
      if (draftSaveTimer) clearTimeout(draftSaveTimer);
      draftSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        draftSaveTimer = 0;
        persistGeneratedDraft().catch(error => {
          console.warn('[🪽위시 RP Manager] API 요약 결과 저장 실패', error);
          setBusy(false, `미적용 결과 저장 실패 · ${error.message}`, 'error');
        });
      }, 450);
    };
    const close = async () => {
      if (state.aiSummaryBusy) { notify('API 요청이 끝난 뒤 닫아 주세요.', 'warn', 3000); return; }
      if (closing) return;
      closing = true;
      if (guideSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer); guideSaveTimer = 0; saveVisibleGuide(); }
      if (draftSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(draftSaveTimer); draftSaveTimer = 0; }
      try {
        await persistGeneratedDraft();
      } catch (error) {
        closing = false;
        setBusy(false, `미적용 결과를 저장하지 못해 창을 닫지 않았습니다. · ${error.message}`, 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (aiDisclosureFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(aiDisclosureFrame);
      aiDisclosureResizeObserver?.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', scheduleAiDisclosureHeights);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      backdrop.remove();
      if (state.aiSummaryDialog === backdrop) state.aiSummaryDialog = null;
    };
    $('.rpcm-ai-close').onclick = close;
    $('#rpcm-ai-cancel').onclick = close;
    $('#rpcm-ai-help-open').onclick = () => { $('#rpcm-ai-help-panel').hidden = false; };
    $('#rpcm-ai-help-close').onclick = () => { $('#rpcm-ai-help-panel').hidden = true; };
    backdrop.addEventListener('click', event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); });
    const onKey = event => {
      if (event.key !== 'Escape' || !document.contains(backdrop)) return;
      const helpPanel = $('#rpcm-ai-help-panel');
      if (helpPanel && !helpPanel.hidden) { helpPanel.hidden = true; return; }
      close();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);

    const renderCurrentStateDiff = () => {
      const base = String(generatedDraft?.baseCurrentState ?? aiFixedSlot(room, 'currentState')?.content ?? '');
      const next = String($('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value || '');
      const diff = buildAiSideBySideDiff(base, next);
      const changedTotal = diff.stats.changed + diff.stats.added + diff.stats.removed;
      const oldSections = parseCurrentStateSections(base);
      const newSections = parseCurrentStateSections(next);
      const oldSectionMap = new Map(oldSections.map(section => [section.title, section.body]));
      const newSectionMap = new Map(newSections.map(section => [section.title, section.body]));
      const changedSections = [...new Set([...oldSectionMap.keys(), ...newSectionMap.keys()])].filter(title => oldSectionMap.get(title) !== newSectionMap.get(title));
      const sectionNames = changedSections.map(title => `‘${title}’`).join(', ');
      const sectionDetails = changedSections.length
        ? `<section class="rpcm-ai-diff-sections rpcm-ai-collapsible" data-ai-collapsible><button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-disclosure-trigger">변경 섹션 ${formatCount(changedSections.length)}개 <b data-ai-disclosure data-closed-label="▾" data-open-label="▴">▾</b></button><div>${esc(sectionNames)}</div></section>`
        : '<span>변경 섹션 없음</span>';
      const rowsHtml = diff.rows.map(row => `<div class="rpcm-ai-diff-row ${row.type}"><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-cell">${esc(row.old)}</div><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-cell">${esc(row.next)}</div></div>`).join('');
      const diffEl = $('#rpcm-ai-state-diff');
      diffEl.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-ai-diff-summary"><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-metrics"><strong>변경 ${formatCount(changedTotal)}줄</strong><span>교체 ${formatCount(diff.stats.changed)}</span><span>추가 ${formatCount(diff.stats.added)}</span><span>삭제 ${formatCount(diff.stats.removed)}</span>${sectionDetails}</div><label class="rpcm-ai-diff-hide"><input type="checkbox" id="rpcm-ai-diff-hide-same" checked> 같은 줄 숨기기</label></div><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-head"><div>기존 현재상태</div><div>API 결과</div></div><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-body hide-same"><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-row same" ${changedTotal ? '' : 'hidden'}><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-cell">${changedTotal ? '변경된 줄만 표시 중' : '변경 없음'}</div><div class="rpcm-ai-diff-cell">${changedTotal ? '초록색은 새 결과' : '기존 내용과 동일'}</div></div>${rowsHtml}</div>`;
      diffEl.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-collapsible]').forEach(container => setAiDisclosureOpen(container, false));
      const hide = diffEl.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-diff-hide-same');
      hide.onchange = () => diffEl.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-diff-body')?.classList.toggle('hide-same', hide.checked);
    };
    const setStateView = view => {
      const diffMode = view === 'diff';
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-edit-tab').classList.toggle('active', !diffMode);
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-diff-tab').classList.toggle('active', diffMode);
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').hidden = diffMode;
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-diff').hidden = !diffMode;
      if (diffMode) renderCurrentStateDiff();
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-state-edit-tab').onclick = () => setStateView('edit');
    $('#rpcm-ai-state-diff-tab').onclick = () => setStateView('diff');

    const activateResultTab = id => {
      const logCard = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card');
      const stateCard = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card');
      const wanted = id === 'currentState' && !stateCard.hidden ? 'currentState' : !logCard.hidden ? 'logSummary' : 'currentState';
      logCard.classList.toggle('is-active', wanted === 'logSummary');
      stateCard.classList.toggle('is-active', wanted === 'currentState');
      $('#rpcm-ai-tab-log').classList.toggle('active', wanted === 'logSummary');
      $('#rpcm-ai-tab-state').classList.toggle('active', wanted === 'currentState');
    };
    const refreshResultTabs = preferred => {
      const logHidden = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden;
      const stateHidden = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden;
      $('#rpcm-ai-tab-log').hidden = logHidden;
      $('#rpcm-ai-tab-state').hidden = stateHidden;
      activateResultTab(preferred || (!logHidden ? 'logSummary' : 'currentState'));
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-tab-log').onclick = () => activateResultTab('logSummary');
    $('#rpcm-ai-tab-state').onclick = () => activateResultTab('currentState');

    const renderFreshLogBlocks = () => {
      const box = $('#rpcm-ai-new-blocks');
      const headings = Array.isArray(generatedDraft?.freshLogHeadings) ? generatedDraft.freshLogHeadings.filter(Boolean) : [];
      if (!box || $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden || !headings.length) {
        if (box) box.hidden = true;
        return;
      }
      const label = generatedDraft?.continuedFromDraft ? '이번 실행에서 추가·교체' : '이번 실행에서 생성';
      box.innerHTML = `<strong>${label} ${formatCount(headings.length)}개</strong>${headings.map(heading => `<span class="rpcm-ai-new-block-chip">${esc(heading)}</span>`).join('')}`;
      box.hidden = false;
    };

    const refreshValidation = () => {
      if (!generatedDraft) { applyBtn.disabled = true; return; }
      renderFreshLogBlocks();
      const targetLog = !$('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').checked;
      const targetState = !$('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').checked;
      const logCheck = validateGeneratedDateSummary($('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value);
      const stateCheck = validateGeneratedCurrentState($('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value);
      const logValidation = $('#rpcm-ai-log-validation');
      const stateValidation = $('#rpcm-ai-state-validation');
      logValidation.textContent = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden ? '' : [...logCheck.errors.map(x => `오류 · ${x}`), ...logCheck.warnings.map(x => `확인 · ${x}`)].join('\n') || `${logCheck.blocks.length}개 날짜 블록 · 적용 시 기존 로그와 병합`;
      logValidation.classList.toggle('error', !!logCheck.errors.length);
      stateValidation.textContent = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden ? '' : [...stateCheck.errors.map(x => `오류 · ${x}`), ...stateCheck.warnings.map(x => `확인 · ${x}`)].join('\n') || `${formatCount(stateCheck.text.length)}자 · 전체 현재상태 교체`;
      stateValidation.classList.toggle('error', !!stateCheck.errors.length);
      applyBtn.disabled = state.aiSummaryBusy || (!targetLog && !targetState) || (targetLog && !!logCheck.errors.length) || (targetState && !!stateCheck.errors.length);
    };
    const handleDraftEdit = () => {
      refreshValidation();
      if (!$('#rpcm-ai-state-diff').hidden) renderCurrentStateDiff();
      queueDraftSave();
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').oninput = handleDraftEdit;
    $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').oninput = handleDraftEdit;
    $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').onchange = handleDraftEdit;
    $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').onchange = handleDraftEdit;
    $('#rpcm-ai-target-log').onchange = () => { scheduleInputPreview(); refreshResumeProgress(); };
    $('#rpcm-ai-target-state').onchange = () => { scheduleInputPreview(); refreshResumeProgress(); };
    $('#rpcm-ai-new-only').onchange = () => { scheduleInputPreview(); refreshResumeProgress(); };
    $('#rpcm-ai-resume-reset').onclick = async () => {
      const targets = [
        ['logSummary', '날짜요약', !!$('#rpcm-ai-target-log').checked],
        ['currentState', '현재상태', !!$('#rpcm-ai-target-state').checked],
      ].filter(([, , selected]) => selected);
      if (!targets.length) {
        notify('먼저 날짜요약이나 현재상태를 하나 이상 선택해 주세요.', 'warn', 3200);
        return;
      }
      const labels = targets.map(([, label]) => label).join('·');
      if (!confirm(`턴 수를 리셋할까요?\n\n${labels}에서 현재 마지막 완료 대화까지 이미 요약한 것으로 처리하고, 위 턴 수를 0으로 바꿉니다. 다음 API 호출은 그다음 대화부터 시작합니다.\n\n외부에서 만든 요약을 직접 붙여넣은 뒤 사용하세요.`)) return;
      try {
        setBusy(true, '현재 마지막 완료 대화를 확인하는 중…');
        const reset = await markAiSummaryCaughtUp(room, targets.map(([id]) => id));
        generatedDraft ||= room.aiSummaryDraft || null;
        if (generatedDraft) {
          generatedDraft.lastTurnKeys ||= {};
          targets.forEach(([id]) => { generatedDraft.lastTurnKeys[id] = String(reset.latestTurn.key); });
          generatedDraft.savedTurnCount = 0;
          generatedDraft.savedAt = reset.markedAt;
          room.aiSummaryDraft = generatedDraft;
          await saveRoom(room);
        }
        $('#rpcm-ai-new-only').checked = true;
        settings.preferUnprocessed = true;
        setTurnCount(0);
        settings.turnCount = 0;
        settings.targets = { logSummary:!!$('#rpcm-ai-target-log').checked, currentState:!!$('#rpcm-ai-target-state').checked };
        saveAiSummarySettings(settings);
        inputPreviewTurns = null;
        refreshResumeInfo();
        await refreshResumeProgress();
        scheduleInputPreview();
        setBusy(false, `턴수 리셋 완료 · 0턴 · ${labels}은 다음 대화부터 요약`, 'success');
        notify(`턴 수를 0으로 리셋했습니다. 다음 대화부터 ${labels}을 생성합니다.`, 'success', 4800);
      } catch (error) {
        setBusy(false, `턴수 리셋 실패 · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`, 'error');
      }
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-save-settings').onclick = () => {
      collectSettings(true);
      updateConnectionSummary();
      setAiDisclosureOpen($('#rpcm-ai-connection'), false);
      setBusy(false, '연결 설정을 이 브라우저에 저장했습니다.', 'success');
      scheduleInputPreview();
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-open-api-settings').onclick = async () => {
      await persistGeneratedDraft();
      backdrop.remove();
      await openUnifiedApiSettingsDialog(room);
      openAiSummaryDialog(room);
    };
    testBtn.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        const next = collectSettings(true);
        if (!confirm('연결 테스트도 짧은 API 호출 1회를 사용합니다. 계속할까요?')) return;
        setBusy(true, 'API 연결을 확인하는 중…');
        const result = await callAiSummaryProvider(next.provider, next, draftSecrets.get(next.provider) || '', '요청에 한 단어로만 답하세요.', '연결됨이라고 답하세요.', 64, () => {}, { feature:'summary', room });
        updateUsage();
        setBusy(false, `연결 성공 · ${result.model} · 이번 호출 예상 ${formatAiCostUsd(result.estimatedCostUsd)}`, 'success');
        updateConnectionSummary();
        setAiDisclosureOpen($('#rpcm-ai-connection'), false);
      } catch (error) { updateUsage(); setBusy(false, `연결 실패 · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`, 'error'); }
    };
    generateBtn.onclick = async () => {
      const previousDraft = generatedDraft;
      const cursorBeforeRun = aiSummaryCursorSnapshot(room);
      let cursorAdvanced = false;
      try {
        if (previousDraft) {
          previousDraft.logSummary = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value;
          previousDraft.currentState = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value;
        }
        const next = collectSettings(true);
        if (currentInputPlan?.risk?.level === 'blocked') throw new Error('가져올 내용이 안전 한도를 넘었습니다. 화면의 권장 최대 턴 이하로 줄여 주세요.');
        setBusy(true, '요약 준비 중…');
        const generationRoom = aiRoomWithPendingDraft(room, previousDraft);
        const freshDraft = await generateAiSummaryDraft(generationRoom, next, draftSecrets.get(next.provider) || '', message => setBusy(true, message));
        setAiFeatureStatus('summary', 'ok', next.provider, freshDraft.model || next.models?.[next.provider] || '', '최근 생성 정상');
        const freshLogHeadings = parseDatedLogBlocks(freshDraft.logSummary).map(block => block.heading).filter(Boolean);
        const previousTargets = previousDraft?.savedTargets || {
          logSummary:!!String(previousDraft?.logSummary || '').trim(),
          currentState:!!String(previousDraft?.currentState || '').trim(),
        };
        const displayTargets = {
          logSummary:!!next.targets.logSummary || !!previousTargets.logSummary,
          currentState:!!next.targets.currentState || !!previousTargets.currentState,
        };
        if (previousDraft) {
          freshDraft.logSummary = mergePendingDateSummary(previousDraft.logSummary, freshDraft.logSummary, room);
          if (!freshDraft.currentState) freshDraft.currentState = String(previousDraft.currentState || '');
          freshDraft.baseCurrentState = String(previousDraft.baseCurrentState ?? aiFixedSlot(room, 'currentState')?.content ?? '');
          freshDraft.lastTurnKeys = { ...(previousDraft.lastTurnKeys || {}), ...(freshDraft.lastTurnKeys || {}) };
          freshDraft.resumeBefore = previousDraft.resumeBefore && typeof previousDraft.resumeBefore === 'object'
            ? structuredClone(previousDraft.resumeBefore)
            : cursorBeforeRun;
          freshDraft.continuedFromDraft = true;
        } else {
          freshDraft.resumeBefore = cursorBeforeRun;
          freshDraft.continuedFromDraft = false;
        }
        freshDraft.freshLogHeadings = freshLogHeadings;
        freshDraft.savedTargets = displayTargets;
        generatedDraft = freshDraft;
        generatedDraft.savedTurnCount = generatedDraft.turns.length;
        upsertAiSummaryHistory(room, generatedDraft);
        renderAiSummaryHistory(generatedDraft.historyId);
        draftClearedAfterApply = false;
        $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden = !displayTargets.logSummary;
        $('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden = !displayTargets.currentState;
        $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value = generatedDraft.logSummary;
        $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value = generatedDraft.currentState;
        $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').checked = !!displayTargets.logSummary;
        $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').checked = !!displayTargets.currentState;
        refreshResultTabs(next.targets.logSummary ? 'logSummary' : next.targets.currentState ? 'currentState' : displayTargets.logSummary ? 'logSummary' : 'currentState');
        setStateView('edit');
        $('#rpcm-ai-draft-banner').hidden = false;
        $('#rpcm-ai-draft-copy').innerHTML = previousDraft
          ? '<strong>새 대화를 기존 미적용 결과에 이어서 갱신했습니다.</strong><br>이번에 생성·교체된 날짜 블록을 아래에서 확인할 수 있습니다.'
          : '<strong>새 요약 결과를 생성해 저장했습니다.</strong><br>이번에 생성된 날짜 블록을 아래에서 확인할 수 있습니다.';
        const runUsage = generatedDraft.usage || {};
        $('#rpcm-ai-run-usage').textContent = `${previousDraft ? '새 대화를 기존 미적용 결과에 이어서 갱신' : '새 요약 생성'} · API ${formatCount(runUsage.calls)}회 · 입력 ${formatCount(runUsage.inputTokens)} / 출력 ${formatCount(runUsage.outputTokens)} 토큰 · 예상 비용 ${runUsage.unpricedCalls ? '단가 미설정' : formatAiCostUsd(runUsage.estimatedCostUsd)}`;
        resultsEl.hidden = false;
        advanceAiSummaryCursorToDraft(room, generatedDraft, Object.entries(displayTargets).filter(([, enabled]) => enabled).map(([id]) => id));
        cursorAdvanced = true;
        setTurnCount(0);
        generateBtn.dataset.inputBlocked = 'true';
        settings.turnCount = 0;
        settings.preferUnprocessed = true;
        $('#rpcm-ai-new-only').checked = true;
        saveAiSummarySettings(settings);
        await persistGeneratedDraft();
        inputPreviewTurns = null;
        refreshResumeInfo();
        await refreshResumeProgress();
        scheduleInputPreview();
        updateUsage();
        const blockNotice = freshLogHeadings.length ? ` · 새 날짜 블록 ${formatCount(freshLogHeadings.length)}개` : '';
        setBusy(false, `${generatedDraft.turns.length}턴 생성 완료${blockNotice} · 처리할 턴 0 · 미적용 결과 저장됨`, 'success');
        refreshValidation();
        resultsEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'start' });
      } catch (error) {
        const failure = classifyAiFailure(error);
        setAiFeatureStatus('summary', 'error', settings.provider, settings.models?.[settings.provider] || '', failure.category, failure.detail);
        if (cursorAdvanced) {
          restoreAiSummaryCursor(room, cursorBeforeRun);
          try { await saveRoom(room); } catch (_) {}
        }
        generatedDraft = previousDraft;
        updateUsage();
        refreshValidation();
        setBusy(false, `생성 실패 · ${failure.category} · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`, 'error');
      }
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-copy-log').onclick = async () => notify(await copyPlainText($('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value) ? '날짜요약 결과를 복사했습니다.' : '복사에 실패했습니다.', 'success', 2800);
    $('#rpcm-ai-copy-state').onclick = async () => notify(await copyPlainText($('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value) ? '현재상태 결과를 복사했습니다.' : '복사에 실패했습니다.', 'success', 2800);
    applyBtn.onclick = async () => {
      try {
        if (!generatedDraft) return;
        generatedDraft.logSummary = $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value;
        generatedDraft.currentState = $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value;
        const selections = { logSummary:!$('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').checked, currentState:!$('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden && $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').checked };
        if (room.pending && !confirm('현재 서버 주입에도 선택한 요약 결과를 즉시 반영합니다. 계속할까요?')) return;
        setBusy(true, '선택한 결과를 저장하는 중…');
        if (draftSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(draftSaveTimer); draftSaveTimer = 0; }
        await applyAiSummaryDraft(room, generatedDraft, selections);
        upsertAiSummaryHistory(room, generatedDraft, { appliedAt:nowIso() });
        renderAiSummaryHistory(generatedDraft.historyId);
        room.aiSummaryDraft = null;
        draftClearedAfterApply = true;
        await saveRoom(room);
        $('#rpcm-ai-draft-banner').hidden = true;
        inputPreviewTurns = null;
        refreshResumeInfo();
        refreshResumeProgress();
        setBusy(false, '적용 완료 · 다음 API 요약은 이번 마지막 대화턴 이후부터 이어집니다.', 'success');
        $('#rpcm-ai-undo').disabled = false;
        notify('API 요약 결과를 적용했습니다. 이어서 요약할 기준점도 저장했습니다.', 'success', 4800);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (error) { setBusy(false, `적용 실패 · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`, 'error'); refreshValidation(); }
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-undo').onclick = async () => {
      if (!confirm('마지막 API 적용 직전의 현재상태와 날짜요약으로 되돌릴까요?')) return;
      try {
        setBusy(true, '마지막 적용을 되돌리는 중…');
        await undoAiSummaryApply(room);
        if (draftSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(draftSaveTimer); draftSaveTimer = 0; }
        room.aiSummaryDraft = null;
        draftClearedAfterApply = true;
        await saveRoom(room);
        generatedDraft = null;
        resultsEl.hidden = true;
        $('#rpcm-ai-draft-banner').hidden = true;
        $('#rpcm-ai-undo').disabled = true;
        refreshResumeInfo();
        refreshResumeProgress();
        setBusy(false, '마지막 API 적용을 되돌렸습니다.', 'success');
        notify('API 적용 전 내용으로 되돌렸습니다.', 'success', 3800);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (error) { setBusy(false, `되돌리기 실패 · ${friendlyAiErrorMessage(error)}`, 'error'); }
    };
    $('#rpcm-ai-discard-draft').onclick = async () => {
      if (!generatedDraft) return;
      if (!confirm('저장된 미적용 요약 결과를 삭제할까요? 기존 현재상태와 날짜요약에는 영향이 없으며, 처리 기준은 이 결과를 만들기 전으로 돌아갑니다.')) return;
      if (draftSaveTimer) { clearTimeout(draftSaveTimer); draftSaveTimer = 0; }
      const resumeBefore = generatedDraft.resumeBefore;
      generatedDraft = null;
      room.aiSummaryDraft = null;
      draftClearedAfterApply = true;
      if (resumeBefore) restoreAiSummaryCursor(room, resumeBefore);
      await saveRoom(room);
      resultsEl.hidden = true;
      $('#rpcm-ai-draft-banner').hidden = true;
      inputPreviewTurns = null;
      refreshResumeInfo();
      await refreshResumeProgress();
      scheduleInputPreview();
      refreshValidation();
      setBusy(false, '미적용 결과를 삭제하고 처리 기준을 생성 전으로 되돌렸습니다.', 'success');
    };
    renderProvider(settings.provider);
    if (generatedDraft) {
      const savedTargets = generatedDraft.savedTargets || {};
      const savedSelections = generatedDraft.savedSelections || {};
      const showLog = generatedDraft.savedTargets ? !!savedTargets.logSummary : !!generatedDraft.logSummary;
      const showState = generatedDraft.savedTargets ? !!savedTargets.currentState : !!generatedDraft.currentState;
      $('#rpcm-ai-log-result-card').hidden = !showLog;
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-result-card').hidden = !showState;
      $('#rpcm-ai-log-result').value = generatedDraft.logSummary;
      $('#rpcm-ai-state-result').value = generatedDraft.currentState;
      $('#rpcm-ai-apply-log').checked = showLog && (generatedDraft.savedSelections ? !!savedSelections.logSummary : true);
      $('#rpcm-ai-apply-state').checked = showState && (generatedDraft.savedSelections ? !!savedSelections.currentState : true);
      refreshResultTabs(showLog ? 'logSummary' : 'currentState');
      setStateView('edit');
      const runUsage = generatedDraft.usage || {};
      $('#rpcm-ai-run-usage').textContent = `저장된 미적용 결과 · ${generatedDraft.savedTurnCount ? `${formatCount(generatedDraft.savedTurnCount)}턴 · ` : ''}API ${formatCount(runUsage.calls)}회 · 입력 ${formatCount(runUsage.inputTokens)} / 출력 ${formatCount(runUsage.outputTokens)} 토큰 · 예상 비용 ${runUsage.unpricedCalls ? '단가 미설정' : formatAiCostUsd(runUsage.estimatedCostUsd)}`;
      resultsEl.hidden = false;
      const savedWhen = generatedDraft.savedAt ? new Date(generatedDraft.savedAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR') : '';
      setBusy(false, `적용하지 않고 닫았던 결과를 복원했습니다.${savedWhen ? ` · ${savedWhen} 저장` : ''}`, 'success');
      refreshValidation();
    }
    updateUsage();
    refreshResumeProgress();
    updateTurnPresetState();
    scheduleInputPreview();
    setTimeout(() => providerEl.focus(), 0);
  }

      #rpcm-fab{position:relative!important;z-index:20!important;display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;gap:6px!important;min-width:0!important;height:34px!important;min-height:34px!important;padding:0 13px!important;border-radius:9px!important;border:1px solid #f472b6!important;background:rgba(244,114,182,.14)!important;color:#f9a8d4!important;box-shadow:none!important;font-family:inherit!important;font-size:13px!important;font-weight:600!important;line-height:1!important;white-space:nowrap!important;cursor:pointer!important;user-select:none!important;transition:background .16s,border-color .16s,color .16s!important;pointer-events:auto!important}

  // Chain only at nested vertical boundaries. Native scrolling handles ordinary movement.
  // One delegated listener also covers dialogs opened later, without scroll observers/re-renders.
  let wishScrollChainingBound=false;
  function bindWishScrollChaining() {
    if(wishScrollChainingBound)return;
    wishScrollChainingBound=true;
    const roots='#rpcm-overlay,[id^="rpcm-"][id$="-backdrop"],#rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog,#rpcm-ai-memory-baseline-dialog,#rpcm-person-core-dialog,#rpcm-person-delete-dialog,#rpcm-person-hidden-dialog,[class*="rpcm-"][class*="-backdrop"],.wish-rpcore-refiner-ui';
    document.addEventListener('wheel',event=>{
      if(event.defaultPrevented||event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey||!event.deltaY||Math.abs(event.deltaX)>Math.abs(event.deltaY))return;
      const target=event.target instanceof Element?event.target:event.target?.parentElement;
      if(!target?.closest(roots))return;
      let boundary=null;
      // Static inline panels share the surrounding Manager boundary; floating dialogs keep their own.
      for(let node=target;node&&node!==document.body;node=node.parentElement){
        if(!node.matches(roots))continue;
        boundary=node;
        if(['fixed','absolute'].includes(getComputedStyle(node).position))break;
      }
      if(!boundary)return;
      const scrollers=[];
      for(let node=target;node;node=node.parentElement){
        if(node.clientHeight>0&&node.scrollHeight>node.clientHeight+1&&/^(auto|scroll)$/.test(getComputedStyle(node).overflowY))scrollers.push(node);
        if(node===boundary)break;
      }
      if(!scrollers.length)return;
      const first=scrollers[0],scale=event.deltaMode===1?18:event.deltaMode===2?first.clientHeight:1;
      let delta=event.deltaY*scale;
      const room=node=>delta>0?Math.max(0,node.scrollHeight-node.clientHeight-node.scrollTop):Math.max(0,node.scrollTop);
      if(room(first)>=Math.abs(delta)+1)return;
      if(!event.cancelable)return;
      event.preventDefault();
      for(const node of scrollers){
        const available=room(node),step=Math.sign(delta)*Math.min(Math.abs(delta),available);
        if(step){const previous=node.scrollTop;node.scrollTop+=step;delta-=node.scrollTop-previous;}
        if(Math.abs(delta)<1)break;
      }
      // Keep the minor horizontal component on diagonal trackpad gestures.
      if(event.deltaX&&first.scrollWidth>first.clientWidth)first.scrollLeft+=event.deltaX*scale;
    },{passive:false});
  }

  // One Wish design layer for migrated UI; older selectors remain only for legacy feature markup.
  function addWishUiStyles() {
    bindWishScrollChaining();
    GM_addStyle(`

  }

  function isElementVisible(el) {
    if (!el?.getBoundingClientRect || !el.isConnected) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width <= 0 || r.height <= 0) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    return cs.display !== 'none' && cs.visibility !== 'hidden';
  }

  function visibleButtonLikes(root = document) {
    const scope = root?.querySelectorAll ? root : document;
    return [...scope.querySelectorAll('button,a,[role="button"]')]
      .filter(el => el.id !== 'rpcm-fab' && !el.closest('#rpcm-overlay') && isElementVisible(el));
  }

  function modelSelectorText(el) {
    return String(el?.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  }

  function findStableCrackHeaderActionRow() {
    // 크랙 요약 메모리 편집기가 안정적으로 사용하는 실제 상단 액션 행을 그대로 사용합니다.
    // 이 컨테이너 안에서만 Manager 위치를 결정하여 본문/좌측 내비/팝오버로 이탈하지 않게 합니다.
    try {
      return document.querySelector('.group\\/header .flex.gap-3.items-center');
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function findModelSelectorButtonInHeader(header) {
    if (!header) return null;
    const badText = /^(manager|lore|기억\s*삽입|설정|요약|파티챗|에피소드)$/i;
    const strongModelWord = /(하이퍼\s*챗|프로\s*챗|프리\s*챗|chat|gpt|claude|gemini|sonnet|opus|flash)/i;
    const candidates = [...header.querySelectorAll('button,[role="button"]')].map(el => {
      if (!el || el === state.fab || el.id === 'summary-editor-btn' || !isElementVisible(el)) return null;
      if (el.closest?.('[role="dialog"],[aria-modal="true"],[role="menu"],[role="listbox"],[data-radix-popper-content-wrapper]')) return null;
      const t = modelSelectorText(el);
      if (!t || badText.test(t)) return null;
      const strong = strongModelWord.test(t);
      const popup = !!el.getAttribute('aria-haspopup') || el.getAttribute('aria-expanded') != null;
      const hasVersionLikeNumber = /\d+(?:\.\d+)?/.test(t);
      if (!strong && !(popup && hasVersionLikeNumber)) return null;
      let score = 0;
      if (strong) score += 200;
      if (hasVersionLikeNumber) score += 70;
      if (popup) score += 35;
      if (t.length >= 3 && t.length <= 32) score += 10;
      return { el, score };
    }).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
    return candidates[0]?.el || null;
  }

  function directChildInsideHost(el, host) {
    if (!el || !host || !host.contains(el)) return null;
    let node = el;
    while (node?.parentElement && node.parentElement !== host) node = node.parentElement;
    return node?.parentElement === host ? node : null;
  }

  function mountManagerNextToModelSelector(fab) {
    const header = findStableCrackHeaderActionRow();
    if (!header || !isElementVisible(header)) return false;
    const modelButton = findModelSelectorButtonInHeader(header);
    if (!modelButton) return false;
    const before = directChildInsideHost(modelButton, header);
    if (!before) return false;

    hideManagerMobileHost();
    fab.classList.remove('rpcm-fallback', 'rpcm-compact-fallback', 'rpcm-mobile-fab');
    for (const prop of ['left','right','top','bottom','position','margin']) fab.style.removeProperty(prop);
    fab.style.setProperty('margin-right', '8px', 'important');

    // 요약 메모리 편집기(📜)와 같은 고정 상단 행에만 삽입합니다.
    // 모델 선택 컴포넌트의 최상위 자식 바로 앞에 넣으므로 모델명/내부 래퍼가 바뀌어도 위치가 유지됩니다.
    if (fab.parentElement !== header || fab.nextSibling !== before) header.insertBefore(fab, before);
    fab.dataset.rpcmPlacement = 'stable-header-before-model';
    managerInlineMounted = true;
    managerFallbackLocked = false;
    return true;
  }

  function createManagerButton() {
    const fab = document.createElement('button');
    fab.type = 'button';
    fab.id = 'rpcm-fab';
    fab.className = 'rpcm-header-monitor';
    fab.innerHTML = '<span class="rpcm-header-monitor-label">WISH</span><span class="rpcm-header-status-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
    fab.onmousedown = event => event.stopPropagation();
    rpcmBindMonitorPress(fab);
    return fab;
  }

  // 기본은 기존 위치를 그대로 사용합니다. 다만 다른 확프/React 재렌더가
  // 이미 꽂힌 Manager 버튼을 실제로 지워버린 것이 감지되면 그 페이지 세션에서는
  // 안전한 body 직속 fixed 위치로 전환합니다. 새로고침/다른 채팅방 이동 시 다시 기존 위치부터 시도합니다.
  let managerPlacementRoute = '';
  let managerInlineMounted = false;
  let managerFallbackLocked = false;
  const MANAGER_MOBILE_HOST_ID = 'rpcm-mobile-button-host';
  const MANAGER_MOBILE_MENU_ATTR = 'data-rpcm-settings-entry';
  let managerMobileHost = null;
  let managerMobileMount = null;
  let managerMobileHostGuard = null;
  let managerMobileRestoreQueued = false;

  function currentManagerPlacementRoute() {
    return `${location.pathname}${location.search}`;
  }

  function resetManagerPlacementGuardIfNeeded() {
    const route = currentManagerPlacementRoute();
    if (route === managerPlacementRoute) return;
    managerPlacementRoute = route;
    managerInlineMounted = false;
    managerFallbackLocked = false;
  }

  function positionManagerSafeFallback(fab) {
    // v0.9.0 안정화: 외부 버튼 DOM의 위치를 따라다니지 않습니다.
    // 다른 확프나 React 재렌더링으로 검색창 위치가 바뀌어도 Manager는
    // 같은 화면 좌표에 고정하여 “버튼이 돌아다니는” 현상을 원천 차단합니다.
    fab.classList.remove('rpcm-compact-fallback');
    fab.style.removeProperty('left');
    fab.style.removeProperty('top');
    fab.style.setProperty('right', '18px', 'important');
    fab.style.setProperty('top', '112px', 'important');
    fab.style.removeProperty('bottom');
  }

  function mountManagerFallback(fab, locked = false) {
    if (!document.body) return false;
    hideManagerMobileHost();
    fab.classList.add('rpcm-fallback');
    if (fab.parentElement !== document.body) document.body.appendChild(fab);
    positionManagerSafeFallback(fab);
    fab.dataset.rpcmPlacement = locked ? 'fixed-safe-recovery' : 'fixed-safe-awaiting-target';
    return true;
  }

  function mobileManagerButtonStyles() {
    return `
      :host{all:initial}
      #mount{width:48px;height:48px;pointer-events:none}
      button{all:unset;box-sizing:border-box!important;position:relative!important;display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;width:48px!important;height:48px!important;min-width:48px!important;min-height:48px!important;border:1px solid #f472b6!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:rgba(36,24,31,.94)!important;color:#f9a8d4!important;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.42)!important;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Pretendard",sans-serif!important;cursor:pointer!important;user-select:none!important;touch-action:manipulation!important;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent!important;pointer-events:auto!important}
      button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #f9a8d4!important;outline-offset:3px!important}
      button[hidden]{display:none!important}
      .rpcm-fab-label{display:none!important}
      .rpcm-fab-wing{display:inline!important;font-size:22px!important;line-height:1!important}
      .rpcm-dot{position:absolute!important;right:5px!important;top:5px!important;width:8px!important;height:8px!important;border:1px solid rgba(24,24,24,.9)!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#f9a8d4!important}
      button.rpcm-armed{background:rgba(85,36,61,.96)!important;border-color:#fb7185!important;color:#fff!important}
      button.rpcm-armed .rpcm-dot{background:#22c55e!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(34,197,94,.16)!important}
      @media(prefers-color-scheme:light){button{background:#fff1f7!important;color:#b84f7e!important;border-color:#df6298!important}}
    `;
  }

  function setImportantStyle(el, name, value) {
    try { el?.style?.setProperty(name, value, 'important'); } catch (_) {}
  }

  function mobileManagerTopOffset() {
    const viewportWidth = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth || 1);
    const viewportHeight = Math.max(1, window.visualViewport?.height || window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 1);
    const size = 48;
    const right = 14;
    const gap = 10;
    let top = 76;
    let obstacles = [];
    try {
      obstacles = visibleButtonLikes(document).filter(el => {
        if (el === state.fab || el.closest?.(`#${MANAGER_MOBILE_HOST_ID}`)) return false;
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return rect.width >= 24 && rect.height >= 24 && rect.width <= 180 && rect.height <= 180 &&
          rect.right >= viewportWidth - 150 && rect.top <= 260 && rect.bottom >= 0;
      }).map(el => el.getBoundingClientRect());
    } catch (_) {}

    for (let pass = 0; pass < 8; pass++) {
      const target = {
        left: viewportWidth - right - size,
        right: viewportWidth - right,
        top,
        bottom: top + size,
      };
      const overlap = obstacles.find(rect =>
        target.left < rect.right + gap && target.right > rect.left - gap &&
        target.top < rect.bottom + gap && target.bottom > rect.top - gap
      );
      if (!overlap) break;
      top = Math.min(Math.max(top + size + gap, overlap.bottom + gap), Math.max(76, viewportHeight - size - 18));
    }
    return Math.round(top);
  }

  function ensureManagerMobileHostGuard() {
    if (managerMobileHostGuard || !document.documentElement) return;
    managerMobileHostGuard = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (!isMobileManagerLayout() || !state.currentChatId || managerMobileHost?.isConnected) return;
      if (managerMobileRestoreQueued) return;
      managerMobileRestoreQueued = true;
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        managerMobileRestoreQueued = false;
        if (!isMobileManagerLayout() || !state.currentChatId) return;
        managerMobileHost = null;
        managerMobileMount = null;
        ensureManagerButton();
      });
    });
    managerMobileHostGuard.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true });
  }

  function ensureManagerMobileHost() {
    if (!document.documentElement) return null;
    if (!managerMobileHost?.isConnected || !managerMobileHost.shadowRoot) {
      const existing = document.getElementById(MANAGER_MOBILE_HOST_ID);
      if (existing?.shadowRoot) {
        managerMobileHost = existing;
        managerMobileMount = existing.shadowRoot.getElementById('mount');
      } else {
        const host = document.createElement('div');
        host.id = MANAGER_MOBILE_HOST_ID;
        host.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        const root = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
        const style = document.createElement('style');
        style.textContent = mobileManagerButtonStyles();
        const mount = document.createElement('div');
        mount.id = 'mount';
        root.append(style, mount);
        document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
        managerMobileHost = host;
        managerMobileMount = mount;
      }
    }

    const top = mobileManagerTopOffset();
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'position', 'fixed');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'width', '48px');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'height', '48px');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'right', '14px');
    managerMobileHost.style.removeProperty('bottom');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'top', `max(calc(env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px) + 12px), ${top}px)`);
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'z-index', '2147483646');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'pointer-events', 'none');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'contain', 'layout style paint');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'visibility', 'visible');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'opacity', '1');
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'display', 'block');
    ensureManagerMobileHostGuard();
    return managerMobileMount;
  }

  function hideManagerMobileHost() {
    if (!managerMobileHost) return;
    setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'display', 'none');
  }

  function normalizedMenuText(value) {
    return String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  }

  function mobileSettingsAnchorRow(label) {
    if (!label) return null;
    const paddedRow = label.closest?.('div[class*="py-"][class*="px-"],li,[role="menuitem"]');
    if (paddedRow) return paddedRow;
    const button = label.closest?.('button,a,[role="button"]');
    if (button) {
      const parent = button.parentElement;
      if (parent && parent !== document.body) {
        const rect = parent.getBoundingClientRect?.();
        if (!rect || (rect.height >= 32 && rect.height <= 92)) return parent;
      }
      return button;
    }
    return label.closest?.('div') || label;
  }

  function createMobileSettingsMenuEntry() {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'px-2.5 h-4 box-content py-[18px]';
    row.setAttribute(MANAGER_MOBILE_MENU_ATTR, '1');
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div role="button" tabindex="0" data-rpcm-open-manager="1" class="w-full flex h-4 items-center justify-between typo-text-base_leading-none_medium space-x-2 [&_svg]:fill-icon_tertiary ring-offset-4 ring-offset-sidebar cursor-pointer">
        <span class="flex space-x-2 items-center min-w-0">
          <span class="rpcm-settings-wing" aria-hidden="true">🪽</span>
          <span class="whitespace-nowrap overflow-hidden text-ellipsis typo-text-sm_leading-none_medium">RP Manager</span>
        </span>
        <span class="rpcm-settings-state-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
      </div>`;
    const open = event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      const action = state.currentRoom?.pending ? openQuickInjectionPanel : openModal;
      action().catch(error => notify(error.message, 'error'));
    };
    const button = row.querySelector('[data-rpcm-open-manager]');
    button?.addEventListener('click', open);
    button?.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return;
      open(event);
    });
    return row;
  }

  function updateMobileSettingsMenuEntry() {
    const row = document.querySelector(`[${MANAGER_MOBILE_MENU_ATTR}="1"]`);
    if (!row) return;
    const armed = !!state.currentRoom?.pending;
    row.classList.toggle('rpcm-menu-armed', armed);
    const button = row.querySelector('[data-rpcm-open-manager]');
    if (button) button.setAttribute('aria-label', armed ? 'RP Manager · 현재 주입 빠른 관리' : 'RP Manager');
  }

  function removeMobileSettingsMenuEntry() {
    document.querySelectorAll(`[${MANAGER_MOBILE_MENU_ATTR}="1"]`).forEach(node => node.remove());
  }

  function ensureMobileSettingsMenuEntry() {
    if (!isMobileManagerLayout() || !state.currentChatId) {
      removeMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
      return false;
    }
    const existing = document.querySelector(`[${MANAGER_MOBILE_MENU_ATTR}="1"]`);
    if (existing) {
      updateMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
      return true;
    }
    const labels = [...document.querySelectorAll('p,span')].filter(node => !node.closest?.('#rpcm-overlay,[data-rpcm-settings-entry="1"]') && isElementVisible(node));
    const summaryLabel = labels.find(node => normalizedMenuText(node.textContent) === '요약 메모리');
    const fallbackLabel = labels.find(node => normalizedMenuText(node.textContent) === '전체 설정');
    const anchorLabel = summaryLabel || fallbackLabel;
    const anchorRow = mobileSettingsAnchorRow(anchorLabel);
    if (!anchorRow?.parentElement) return false;
    const row = createMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
    anchorRow.parentElement.insertBefore(row, anchorRow.nextSibling);
    updateMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
    return true;
  }

  function scheduleMobileSettingsMenuEntry(delay = 90) {
    clearTimeout(state.mobileMenuTimer);
    state.mobileMenuTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      state.mobileMenuTimer = null;
      ensureMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
    }, Math.max(0, Number(delay) || 0));
  }

  function bindMobileSettingsMenuEntry() {
    if (state.mobileMenuBound) return;
    state.mobileMenuBound = true;
    document.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (!isMobileManagerLayout() || !state.currentChatId) return;
      scheduleMobileSettingsMenuEntry(80);
      setTimeout(() => scheduleMobileSettingsMenuEntry(0), 280);
      setTimeout(() => scheduleMobileSettingsMenuEntry(0), 650);
    }, true);
  }

  function mountManagerMobile(fab) {
    const mount = ensureManagerMobileHost();
    if (!mount) return false;
    // 모바일 버튼은 페이지/다른 확프 CSS가 닿지 않는 Shadow DOM 안에 둡니다.
    // 호스트는 body 밖 documentElement 직속이라 React가 body를 다시 그려도 유지됩니다.
    fab.classList.add('rpcm-fallback', 'rpcm-mobile-fab');
    fab.classList.remove('rpcm-compact-fallback');
    fab.style.removeProperty('bottom');
    if (fab.parentNode !== mount) mount.appendChild(fab);
    fab.dataset.rpcmPlacement = 'shadow-mobile';
    managerInlineMounted = false;
    return true;
  }

  function updateFab() {
    if (!state.fab) {
      updateQuickInjectionTrigger();
      return;
    }
    const visible = !!state.currentChatId && !state.modal;
    state.fab.hidden = !visible;
    const armed = !!state.currentRoom?.pending;
    state.fab.classList.toggle('rpcm-armed', armed);
    state.fab.title = armed ? `🪽위시 RP Manager · 자동 유지 중 (${pendingProgressText(state.currentRoom.pending)})` : '🪽위시 RP Manager';
    state.fab.setAttribute('aria-label', state.fab.title);
    if (managerMobileHost?.isConnected && isMobileManagerLayout()) {
      setImportantStyle(managerMobileHost, 'visibility', visible ? 'visible' : 'hidden');
    }
    updateMobileSettingsMenuEntry();
    updateQuickInjectionTrigger();
  }

  function ensureManagerButton() {
    resetManagerPlacementGuardIfNeeded();
    if (!state.currentChatId) {
      if (state.fab) { state.fab.rpcmCancelHold?.(); state.fab.hidden = true; }
      hideManagerMobileHost();
      return false;
    }

    const fab = state.fab || createManagerButton();
    const mobileLayout = isMobileManagerLayout();
    fab.classList.toggle('rpcm-mobile-fab', mobileLayout);

    if (mobileLayout && !findModelSelectorButtonInHeader(findStableCrackHeaderActionRow())) {
      hideManagerMobileHost();
      if (fab.isConnected) { fab.rpcmCancelHold?.(); fab.remove(); }
      state.fab = fab;
      scheduleMobileSettingsMenuEntry(0);
      updateFab();
      return true;
    }

    removeMobileSettingsMenuEntry();

    // 데스크톱은 모델 선택 버튼(예: "프로챗 1.0") 바로 왼쪽 한 자리만 사용합니다.
    // 검색창/메뉴를 위치 기준으로 따라다니지 않으므로 Manager가 화면 여기저기로 이동하지 않습니다.
    if (mountManagerNextToModelSelector(fab)) {
      state.fab = fab;
      updateFab();
      return true;
    }

    // 상단 액션 행/모델 선택기가 아직 렌더링되지 않았다면 엉뚱한 곳에 임시로 띄우지 않습니다.
    // 요약 메모리 편집기와 같은 실제 헤더가 준비될 때까지 DOM 밖에서 대기하고, 다음 observer/주기 체크에서 정확한 자리로만 삽입합니다.
    managerInlineMounted = false;
    managerFallbackLocked = false;
    if (fab.isConnected) { fab.rpcmCancelHold?.(); fab.remove(); }
    fab.dataset.rpcmPlacement = 'waiting-stable-header';
    state.fab = fab;
    updateFab();
    return false;
  }

  function createFab() {
    // 기본 위치를 먼저 시도하고, 충돌로 실제 삭제되는 경우에만 독립 폴백으로 전환합니다.
    ensureManagerButton();
  }

  async function refreshCarrierCapacityPreview(room, force = false) {
    if (!room) return;
    const roomId = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (room.pending) {
      state.carrierCapacityPreview = { roomId, originalChars:Number(room.pending.originalChars || 0), originalText:String(room.pending.originalText || ''), loading:false, error:'', checkedAt:Date.now() };
      state.capacityUiRefresh?.();
      return;
    }
    const previous = state.carrierCapacityPreview;
    if (!force && previous && previous.roomId === roomId && (previous.loading || (!previous.error && Date.now() - Number(previous.checkedAt || 0) < 10000))) return;
    state.carrierCapacityPreview = { roomId, originalChars:0, loading:true, error:'', checkedAt:Date.now() };
    state.capacityUiRefresh?.();
    try {
      const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), 20);
      const latestAssistant = recent.find(message => messageRoleOf(message) === 'assistant');
      if (!latestAssistant) throw new Error('최신 AI 답변 없음');
      const raw = String(messageTextOf(latestAssistant) || '');
      if (!raw) throw new Error('최신 AI 답변 원문 없음');
      const original = stripOurContextBlock(raw).text || raw;
      if (String(state.currentRoom?.chatId || '') !== roomId) return;
      state.carrierCapacityPreview = {
        roomId,
        messageId:String(messageIdOf(latestAssistant) || ''),
        originalChars:String(original).length,
        originalText:String(original),
        loading:false,
        error:'',
        checkedAt:Date.now(),
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (String(state.currentRoom?.chatId || '') !== roomId) return;
      state.carrierCapacityPreview = { roomId, originalChars:0, loading:false, error:String(error?.message || '확인 실패'), checkedAt:Date.now() };
    }
    state.capacityUiRefresh?.();
  }

  function refreshUserCanonMemoBadge(room) {
    const badge=state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-user-memo-badge');
    if (!badge || state.currentRoom !== room) return;
    const count=activeUserCanonMemos(room).length;
    badge.hidden=!count;badge.textContent=count>99?'99+':String(count);
    state.modal.querySelector('#rpcm-user-memo-open')?.setAttribute('aria-label',`사용자 메모 열기 · 활성 ${count}개`);
  }

  function invalidateUserCanonMemoPackets(room) {
    for (const task of Object.keys(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS)) manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room,task));
    if (room.fullRebuildJob?.jobId) fullRebuildRuntime.files.delete(room.fullRebuildJob.jobId);
  }

  async function commitUserCanonMemos(room,change) {
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    syncOpenModalIntoRoom(room);
    const candidate=structuredClone(room);
    change(normalizeUserCanonMemos(candidate));
    await saveRoom(candidate);
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('저장 중 방이 바뀌었습니다. 현재 방을 다시 확인해 주세요.');
    rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,candidate);
    invalidateUserCanonMemoPackets(room);
    refreshUserCanonMemoBadge(room);
  }

  function openUserCanonMemoDialog(room) {
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) return;
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-user-memo-backdrop')?.remove();
    ensureUserCanonMemoStyles();
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-user-memo-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML='<section class="rpcm-user-memo-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="사용자 메모"><header class="rpcm-user-memo-head"><div><h2>사용자 메모</h2><small>이 방의 AI 기억 작업에만 전달됩니다. RP 주입문에는 넣지 않습니다.</small></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-user-memo-close" data-memo-act="close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">×</button></header><main class="rpcm-user-memo-body"><button type="button" data-memo-act="add">＋ 메모 추가</button><div class="rpcm-user-memo-warning" data-memo-warning hidden></div><div class="rpcm-user-memo-list" data-memo-list></div><div data-memo-editor></div></main><footer class="rpcm-user-memo-foot"><small>사용자 원문 그대로 저장 · 메모당 최대 2,000자</small><button type="button" data-memo-act="close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="text">닫기</button></footer></section>';
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    const list=backdrop.querySelector('[data-memo-list]'),editor=backdrop.querySelector('[data-memo-editor]');
    const expanded=new Set();let editing=null,editingDraft='',busy=false;
    const close=()=>{if(!busy)backdrop.remove();};
    const render=()=>{
      rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
      const memos=normalizeUserCanonMemos(room);
      const active=activeUserCanonMemos(room),totalChars=active.reduce((n,memo)=>n+memo.text.length,0);
      const warning=backdrop.querySelector('[data-memo-warning]');
      warning.hidden=totalChars<=USER_CANON_MEMO_WARN_CHARS;
      warning.textContent=warning.hidden?'':`활성 메모 총 ${formatCount(totalChars)}자입니다. 긴 메모는 패킷·AI 입력 한도를 초과할 수 있습니다. 내용은 잘라내지 않습니다.`;
      list.innerHTML=memos.length?memos.map((memo,index)=>{const id=String(memo?.id||''),on=memo?.enabled!==false;return `<article class="rpcm-user-memo-card${on?'':' is-off'}${expanded.has(id)?' is-expanded':''}" data-memo-id="${esc(id)}"><div class="rpcm-user-memo-row"><label><input type="checkbox" data-memo-act="toggle" ${on?'checked':''}> 활성</label><strong>메모 ${index+1}</strong><small>${esc(memo?.updatedAt||'')}</small><span class="spacer"></span><button type="button" data-memo-act="expand">${expanded.has(id)?'접기':'펼치기'}</button><button type="button" data-memo-act="edit">편집</button><button type="button" data-memo-act="delete">삭제</button></div><p class="rpcm-user-memo-preview">${esc(String(memo?.text||''))}</p></article>`;}).join(''):'<div class="rpcm-user-memo-empty">이 방의 사용자 메모가 없습니다.</div>';
      if(editing!==null){editor.innerHTML=`<div class="rpcm-user-memo-editor"><strong>${editing==='new'?'새 메모':'메모 편집'}</strong><textarea data-memo-text aria-label="사용자 메모 원문">${esc(editingDraft)}</textarea><small data-memo-count></small><div class="rpcm-user-memo-editor-actions"><button type="button" data-memo-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="primary" data-memo-act="save">저장</button></div></div>`;editor.querySelector('[data-memo-count]').textContent=`${editor.querySelector('textarea').value.length} / ${USER_CANON_MEMO_MAX_CHARS}자`;}
      else editor.replaceChildren();
    };
    render();
    backdrop.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(event.target.matches('[data-memo-text]')){editingDraft=event.target.value;const count=editor.querySelector('[data-memo-count]');count.textContent=`${editingDraft.length} / ${USER_CANON_MEMO_MAX_CHARS}자`;count.classList.toggle('rpcm-user-memo-warning',editingDraft.length>USER_CANON_MEMO_MAX_CHARS);}});
    backdrop.addEventListener('click',async event=>{
      if(event.target===backdrop){close();return;}
      const action=event.target.closest('[data-memo-act]')?.dataset.memoAct;if(!action||busy)return;
      if(action==='close'){close();return;}
      if(action==='add'){editing='new';editingDraft='';render();editor.querySelector('textarea')?.focus();return;}
      if(action==='cancel'){editing=null;render();return;}
      const id=event.target.closest('[data-memo-id]')?.dataset.memoId;
      if(action==='expand'){expanded.has(id)?expanded.delete(id):expanded.add(id);render();return;}
      if(action==='edit'){editing=id;editingDraft=String(normalizeUserCanonMemos(room).find(item=>item?.id===id)?.text||'');render();editor.querySelector('textarea')?.focus();return;}
      if(action==='delete'&&!confirm('이 사용자 메모를 삭제할까요? 다른 기억 데이터는 삭제되지 않습니다.'))return;
      if(action==='save'){
        const value=editor.querySelector('[data-memo-text]')?.value||'';
        if(!value.trim()){notify('빈 메모는 저장할 수 없습니다.','warn');return;}
        if(value.length>USER_CANON_MEMO_MAX_CHARS){notify(`메모는 ${USER_CANON_MEMO_MAX_CHARS}자 이하로 작성해 주세요.`,'warn');return;}
        busy=true;
        try{await commitUserCanonMemos(room,memos=>{if(editing==='new')memos.push({id:`memo_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`,text:value,enabled:true,createdAt:nowIso(),updatedAt:nowIso()});else{const memo=memos.find(item=>item?.id===editing);if(!memo)throw new Error('편집할 메모를 찾지 못했습니다.');memo.text=value;memo.updatedAt=nowIso();}});editing=null;render();notify('사용자 메모를 저장했습니다.','success');}
        catch(error){notify(`메모 저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}finally{busy=false;}return;
      }
      if(action==='delete'){
        busy=true;try{await commitUserCanonMemos(room,memos=>{const index=memos.findIndex(item=>item?.id===id);if(index<0)throw new Error('삭제할 메모를 찾지 못했습니다.');memos.splice(index,1);});if(editing===id)editing=null;render();notify('사용자 메모를 삭제했습니다.','success');}catch(error){notify(`메모 삭제 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}finally{busy=false;}
      }
    });
    backdrop.addEventListener('change',async event=>{if(!event.target.matches('[data-memo-act="toggle"]')||busy)return;const id=event.target.closest('[data-memo-id]')?.dataset.memoId;const enabled=event.target.checked;busy=true;try{await commitUserCanonMemos(room,memos=>{const memo=memos.find(item=>item?.id===id);if(!memo)throw new Error('메모를 찾지 못했습니다.');memo.enabled=enabled;memo.updatedAt=nowIso();});render();}catch(error){event.target.checked=!enabled;notify(`메모 상태 저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}finally{busy=false;}});
    backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();};
  }

  async function openModal() {
    const chatId = getChatIdFromPath();
    if (!chatId) {
      notify('채팅방 화면에서만 사용할 수 있습니다.', 'warn');
      return;
    }
    const roomReady = prepareManagerOpenRoom(chatId);
    if (roomReady?.then) await roomReady;
    manualRestoreDrafts(state.currentRoom);
    if (state.modal) closeModal();

    updateViewportMetrics();
    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.id = 'rpcm-overlay';
    overlay.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    overlay.classList.add('rpcm-initializing');
    // 호환 모드: 바깥 영역 클릭을 가로채지 않습니다. 닫기 버튼으로만 닫습니다.
    state.modal = overlay;
    state.modalOpening = true;
    state.modalOpenDirty = false;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    try {
      renderModal();
      scheduleStoryReviewCountRefresh(state.currentRoom);
      applyModalPosition();
    } catch (error) {
      closeModal();
      throw error;
    }
    let openingPasses = 0;
    const finishOpening = () => {
      if (state.modal !== overlay) return;
      if (state.modalOpenDirty && openingPasses < 2) {
        openingPasses += 1;
        state.modalOpenDirty = false;
        try { renderModal(); applyModalPosition(); }
        catch (error) { closeModal(); notify(`화면을 여는 중 문제가 발생했습니다: ${error.message}`, 'error'); return; }
        requestAnimationFrame(finishOpening);
        return;
      }
      state.modalOpening = false;
      state.modalOpenDirty = false;
      overlay.style.visibility = '';
      updateFab();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => overlay.classList.remove('rpcm-initializing'));
      refreshCarrierCapacityPreview(state.currentRoom, true).catch(() => {});
      void refreshLongTermNoticeInventory(state.currentRoom);
      void auditManualIntegrityOnOpen(state.currentRoom);
    };
    // 스크롤/탭 복원 다음 프레임에 바로 표시합니다. 추가 표시 대기는 두지 않습니다.
    requestAnimationFrame(finishOpening);
  }

  function closeModal() {
    const workContent = state.modal?.querySelector('.rpcm-work-content');
    if (workContent && !state.modalOpening) state.modalScrollByView.set(`${state.currentRoom?.chatId || ''}:${state.workView || 'overview'}`, workContent.scrollTop);
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-start-backdrop')?.remove();
    const detached = document.querySelector('#rpcm-detached-backdrop');
    if (typeof detached?._rpcmClose === 'function') detached._rpcmClose(); else detached?.remove();
    document.querySelector('.rpcm-focus-shade')?.remove();
    const preview = document.querySelector('#rpcm-preview-backdrop');
    const importer = document.querySelector('#rpcm-import-backdrop');
    if (typeof preview?._rpcmClose === 'function') preview._rpcmClose(); else preview?.remove();
    if (typeof importer?._rpcmClose === 'function') importer._rpcmClose(); else importer?.remove();
    rpcmHideTip();
    state.modal?.remove();
    state.modal = null;
    state.modalOpening = false;
    state.modalOpenDirty = false;
    state.capacityUiRefresh = null;
    updateFab();
  }

  let deferredDialogRender = null;
  function renderModalIfOpen() {
    const blocking = document.querySelector('#rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog,#rpcm-compression-backdrop');
    if (blocking && state.modal) {
      if (!deferredDialogRender) {
        deferredDialogRender = new MutationObserver(() => {
          if(document.querySelector('#rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog,#rpcm-compression-backdrop'))return;
          deferredDialogRender.disconnect();deferredDialogRender=null;
          renderModalIfOpen();
        });
        // Top-level dialog removal only: scrolling/text changes never trigger this observer.
        deferredDialogRender.observe(document.body,{childList:true});
      }
      return;
    }
    if (state.modal) {
      if (state.modalOpening) { state.modalOpenDirty = true; return; }
      renderModal();
      refreshCarrierCapacityPreview(state.currentRoom).catch(() => {});
    }
    if (state.quickPanel) renderQuickInjectionPanel();
    updateFab();
  }

  function applyModalPosition() {
    const wrap = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-modal-wrap');
    if (!wrap || isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
    const p = state.modalPos || loadModalPosition();
    if (!p) return;
    const maxLeft = Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - wrap.offsetWidth);
    const maxTop = Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - Math.min(wrap.offsetHeight, window.innerHeight - 8));
    wrap.style.left = `${Math.max(0, Math.min(maxLeft, p.left))}px`;
    wrap.style.top = `${Math.max(0, Math.min(maxTop, p.top))}px`;
    wrap.style.right = 'auto';
  }

  function bindModalDrag() {
    const overlay = state.modal;
    const wrap = overlay?.querySelector('#rpcm-modal-wrap');
    const header = overlay?.querySelector('.rpcm-header');
    if (!wrap || !header || isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
    applyModalPosition();

    header.onmousedown = (e) => {
      if (e.button !== 0) return;
      if (e.target.closest('button,input,textarea,a,summary,details')) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      const startLeft = rect.left;
      const startTop = rect.top;
      const dx = e.clientX - startLeft;
      const dy = e.clientY - startTop;
      const maxLeft = Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width);
      const maxTop = Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - Math.min(rect.height, window.innerHeight - 8));
      let nextLeft = startLeft;
      let nextTop = startTop;
      let frameId = 0;
      header.classList.add('rpcm-dragging');
      wrap.classList.add('rpcm-drag-active');
      wrap.style.right = 'auto';

      const renderMove = () => {
        frameId = 0;
        wrap.style.transform = `translate3d(${nextLeft - startLeft}px, ${nextTop - startTop}px, 0)`;
      };
      const move = (ev) => {
        nextLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxLeft, ev.clientX - dx));
        nextTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxTop, ev.clientY - dy));
        if (!frameId) frameId = requestAnimationFrame(renderMove);
      };
      const up = () => {
        document.removeEventListener('mousemove', move, true);
        document.removeEventListener('mouseup', up, true);
        if (frameId) cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
        wrap.style.transform = 'none';
        wrap.style.left = `${nextLeft}px`;
        wrap.style.top = `${nextTop}px`;
        header.classList.remove('rpcm-dragging');
        wrap.classList.remove('rpcm-drag-active');
        saveModalPosition(nextLeft, nextTop);
      };
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', move, true);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', up, true);
    };
  }

  function statusForChars(chars, maxChars, payloadBytes = 0) {
    const ratio = maxChars ? chars / maxChars : 0;
    const byteRatio = payloadBytes ? payloadBytes / APP.safeCarrierPayloadBytes : 0;
    if (chars > maxChars || byteRatio > 1) return { cls: 'bad', label: '한도 초과', color: '#ef4444', ratio: 1 };
    if (chars > APP.safeChars || ratio > .9 || byteRatio > .9) return { cls: 'warn', label: '한도 근접', color: '#f59e0b', ratio:Math.max(ratio, byteRatio) };
    return { cls: '', label: '여유', color: '#22c55e', ratio };
  }
